## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 09 — ADDED AND AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 09 OUTLINE PHASE, in its own words

**The four decisions above were taken by the Volume 08 close phase and this phase inherited all four without moving any of them. The Volume 09 line, the voice, the calendar and the cast are exactly as that phase wrote them and exactly as its review repair pass corrected them. This phase is the sixth phase in this repository that has been permitted to decide, and it took five more, on the date in the phase ledger for `workspace/volume-09/outline`, and it struck the candidate line, which the close had audited and not adopted, and it wrote the volume's entry in the volume list above, which the close had deliberately left empty because an entry there is a line and a line is the outline phase's to write. They are written here, in the series outline, so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost. Nothing else in this file moved, the ending did not move, no new final enemy was introduced, and the antagonist ladder and the relationship milestones are untouched. This block sits after the block above and before the volume list, and a reader who wants the series and not the argument should skip all five blocks.**

**AND THE FOUR THINGS THIS PHASE DID ARE ON DISK AND NAMED HERE, because the phase that wrote the four decisions above was caught by a review describing work it had not done, and a block that claims an act is worth nothing to the writer who comes after it unless it was.** `outline/volume-09.md`; `outline/batches/volume-09-batch-0001.md`, ten cards; this block; and the four state files repointed at `workspace/volume-09/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, which is written. **No chapter was written. No day was moved. `outline/ending.md` was not opened. `bible/power-system.md` was not edited, and §51 is unchanged, which is the strongest single fact about this volume's calendar layer: the outline phase that was permitted to amend it chose not to, and the eighteen round-figure traps and the twenty-one interval anchors and the fifty-row day table in it are confirmed rather than corrected by this phase's own run.**

**DECISION FIVE — THE LINE. THE CLOSE'S CANDIDATE IS STRUCK, AND WHAT REPLACES IT IS ADOPTED, AND THE AUDIT WAS RE-RUN BEFORE THE ADOPTION.** The standing test is at `outline/volume-07.md` §19: a line is made of words the manuscript has never used AND of things the manuscript has never seen, and a line that is only the first collides with a page that already means something else.

**The candidate the close audited is struck as a line, and three things in it are kept as sources of pressure, and the strike is published at `outline/volume-09.md` §19.4 rather than made silently. It is struck for three reasons and the three are the finding: its question is spent, because *Who is a fence for* is on a page in Chapter 0396 unanswered and the candidate is that question in a longer coat; it has no instrument, and a line that says there is no form for the thing has decided the volume's question before the first chapter and then has nothing to put in fifty of them, and the whole of this manuscript's method is that the only way a thing gets into the world is a person in a room in daylight with a date on the door doing it; and it puts the man who cannot move a thing at the centre, and the standing prohibition is that he is not asked, not told and not defeated, and a line whose engine is asking those two men is a line this book has decided four volumes running it may not write.**

**What replaces them, in one sentence: *a boundary has to be said out loud before it is a boundary, and the only instrument four hundred miles has for saying one is a sheet with four places under a heading, and the third of the four places is a person, and the two men the line stands between are not going to be asked, and the only page in the world that says where the line runs is going away down a road that nobody in this city can be shown one person on.*** Its load-bearing nouns are *boundary*, *line*, *place*, *person*, *page*, *form*, *road*, *asked* and *man*, and **every one of them is in the high column of this manuscript's own vocabulary and not one of them is at zero, and that is the finding and it is the same finding that made Volume 08's line work.** The second half of the test is therefore carried by three things this manuscript has never had on a page, and they are named at `outline/volume-09.md` §19.5 so that a writer can check them and a reviewer can fail them: **a page that asks a person to put themselves on it; a column left empty on purpose by a room rather than by one person; and a line that comes back moved with no column on the page for the person who moved it.** The third of those is the volume's discipline as well as its newness, because **no chapter of Volume 09 traces the move or asks who made it.**

**And the audit found one more thing, and it is about instruments, and it is a finding against an inherited correction. Every figure in the thirteen-row vocabulary row the close published was re-measured on all 400 chapter files, and the definition that row publishes — a token bounded by whitespace, punctuation or a line end — does not say what a hyphen and an apostrophe are, so five readings of it were run and all five are published. The reading that reproduces the most of the published row is the one where a letter, a hyphen and an apostrophe all continue a word, and it reproduces nine of thirteen exactly. The four that do not are each exactly one high, and each is the figure the close's own first edition printed, and the four figures the close's review repair pass subtracted one from reproduce on no reading this phase could construct. So: the first edition was right and the correction moved four figures away from a reproducible value, and the first edition is not reinstated by this phase and the corrected figures are not deleted from this file. Both stand and the table is at `outline/volume-09.md` §19.2. The tell is the shape of the disagreement: the nine that agree are the nine where the unstated flag cannot make any difference, and the four that differ are the four where it can. A table whose disagreements cluster on exactly the tokens the unstated flag could touch is a table that was read twice, and a table that was read twice is a table whose instrument was not published.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses a candidate it never adopted and gains a line whose subject is a column rather than a person or a journey, and the cost is that this volume's plot is the least dramatic of the run so far: a sheet arrives, a sheet is read out, a room decides, a sheet goes out, a sheet comes back different, and the only event in fifty days that is not a document is a man saying a thing out loud in a room and a man refusing a rule out loud in the same room ten days later. The series also loses a placement of Ivenn, which was already a debt and is not a new loss, and a relationship milestone, which was not going to be paid in this volume whatever the line was. The antagonist ladder, the relationship milestones and `outline/ending.md` are untouched and no new final enemy is introduced.**

**DECISION SIX — THERE IS NO EIGHTH NOTICE IN THIS FLOOD, AND VOLUME 09 SENDS NONE, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE COST.** The instrument that brings about nine people to a room in this manuscript is a sheet of paper with a day on it and a place on it and nothing at the top of it, going out four days before the room, unsigned, to a place and not to a house. It is the oldest instrument in the book and it is the one this repository has written about most and checked least, because a check of numerals cannot see it.

**And there is not going to be an eighth one, and the page that says so is `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0389.md:35`, where a man working boards at the bottom of this city said out loud that the sheet that had just gone past him was the last sheet of paper in this flood that has gone past him. `state/volume-08-close.md` §7.3 records the wider version of the same fact. Both stand. This phase takes the page as binding and not the record, because a page is a page and a record is a witness, and the one difference between them is the difference between a sheet and every sheet, and a phase that quietly widened a man's sentence to make its own life easier would be doing the thing this book is about.**

**So Volume 09 sends zero notices, and that is not a shortage and not a lapse: it is the instrument being spent. Every room in the volume is a room about nine people were already in, and the day on the door is still written in chalk, and the four days of notice is not available and `outline/volume-09.md` §14.4 carries the empty notice table so that a batch does not fill it.**

**And what is lost with it is named, because a cost that is not named is a cost a chapter will spend by accident. The four days are not a courtesy and they are not only transport: the four days are the cooling, and the cooling is what a room has in which to find out what it is being asked, and a room given a sheet to read out on the morning it arrives is a room that decides before it has understood. The volume's midpoint is therefore a decision made faster than it should be, and the chapter says so in plain narration and says it once. And what a summoned room could have done and this one cannot is the thing a summoned room is for: it could have sent somebody to ask somebody a question in advance. There is no instrument in this volume for asking anybody anything in advance of anything, and that is why the man of about fifty-seven is not asked, and it is not a decision anybody took about him.**

**What it costs, plainly. The volume's decision row falls from Volume 08's eight to four, and that fall is a consequence and not a virtue and is counted honestly in the rotation table with the reason beside it. A volume that is short of decisions and knows why is a different thing from a volume that is short of decisions. And the gain is the largest single change in this series' pressure rotation in four volumes: character and relationship goes to seventeen chapters out of fifty, against Volume 08's twelve and Volume 07's seven, because a notice is the thing that used to put a person in a room and a volume with none of them has to get its people from the faces in a room that were already there.**

**DECISION SEVEN — THE FOUR PLACES ARE A PLACE, A LINE, A DAY AND A PERSON, THE THIRD OF THEM IS A PERSON, AND THE SHEET IS NEITHER A MYSTERY NOR A DEFEAT.** A third four-place form comes onto a page in this flood, out of the same office as the return, on the same quarterly round, and it is the first of the three that has a place for the person in it. The return is a district, a population, a rota and a share and has no place for the person. The Crown's schedule is a date, a gate, a holder and the days and stands and is correct and is not opened. **The three are never joined, never conflated and never compared out loud, and a keeper is not a holder and the word is defined by nobody.**

**What is decided: the third place is a person; the room's decision on day 425 is to leave it empty; four lines are drawn by four people; the sheet goes out on day 438 with four lines and nothing in the third column; it comes back with one of the four moved. What is not decided and may not be: who moved it. No chapter traces the move, investigates it, guesses at it, reports it to a room, or says in any mouth that anybody moved it. No document in this volume is defeated, a document that goes out and comes back is the ordinary end of a document and is not a defeat, and this one comes back different and nobody can prove it is different.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses a document that could have been exposed and gets a document that is answered and unanswered at once, and the volume's antagonist is a method for the third time running: a page that says where a line runs, put on a table in a room, read out, and answered. Nobody is defeated by it and nothing is solved by it, and the woman whose line moves is in no chapter after the one in which she is asked and is never going to be asked about it, because a chapter that asks her is a chapter that resolves it.**

**DECISION EIGHT — THE NEW QUESTION IS THE THIRD OF THREE, IT IS ASKED ON DAY 445 BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE, AND IT IS NOT ANSWERED.** *What is a line for, on a page, when the only thing anybody will use it for is to move it.* Asked to the room and not to anybody, in the room over a market, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door. Nobody answers it; about four of the nine had an answer and about four said nothing, and the chapter says so in the next line, exactly as Chapter 0346 says it of the question of day 346 and Chapter 0396 says it of the question of day 396.

**The rhyme is a decision and not an accident, and it is the only structural rhyme in three volumes: a person, then a thing, then a line, asked three times by one woman in one building about a hundred days apart, each one harder, each one got less, and the whole of it is that nobody in this series has yet been able to ask a question a room can answer. A later volume may ask it again, in a mouth, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door. A later volume may not answer it, and a batch may not answer it, and neither may an outline.**

**What it costs, plainly. Nothing except the risk of a rhyme becoming a manner, and the outline meets that by prohibiting the chapter from observing the rhyme at all: no chapter may say that the three rhyme and no chapter may improve the rhyme and no chapter may say that the rhyme is the point.**

**DECISION NINE — THE POOL THE CLOSE PUBLISHED IS TWELVE NAMES AND ALL TWELVE ALREADY HAVE PEOPLE, AND VOLUME 09'S ONE NEW PERSON IS TAKEN FROM OUTSIDE IT.** The close's decision four published twelve descriptors as the pool a new person may be taken from. **The twelve reproduce exactly on 400 chapter files on this phase's instrument, so the pool is correct, and every one of the twelve is already a person with a page: the woman of twenty-four who refused the naming of a commons, the man of thirty-six of a market town, the woman of thirty-three at a bread board, the woman of sixty-one with the shrine and the bench, the boy of eleven who came in at about the eighth hour, the woman of twenty-eight who keeps the days in her own hand, the woman of sixty in the felt hat, the man of sixty-three who farms the half of the holding and is in this volume, the man of sixty-four who digs a ditch he is not paid for, the woman of twenty-one with the pen who is one of the four and is on a road, the man of thirty-three who keeps a room over a market and is the room this volume is set in, and the man of twenty-one who walked nine places in three days.** A pool of twelve occupied rooms is not a pool, and the four hundred-file figure is printed beside each one at `outline/volume-09.md` §7.2 so that no later phase draws a returning person out of it.

**So Volume 09 adds one new person and one only: a woman of about fifty-four, at three occurrences in one file across four hundred chapter files and at zero in Volume 07's fifty and Volume 08's fifty, taken from a published set of forty-seven descriptors that stand at zero in both of the last two volumes, and she is put on a page by being asked, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, where her line is. One and not two, because a volume whose instrument is a sheet read out to the nine who were already in the room does not need a room full of strangers, and a volume that adds three is a volume filling a room.** Her second handle — a length of blue thread tied round her wrist, which is how she has always known a length by — is invented on the page in Chapter 0420.

**AND THE CROWDED FOUR STILL MAY NOT BE GIVEN TO A NEW PERSON BY ANYBODY, and all four reproduce on 400 files: *woman of about thirty-four* 362 in 147, *man of about thirty-four* 341 in 139, *man of about forty-four* 162 in 85, *man of about thirty-nine* 177 in 93. And the man of about thirty-seven is at zero in Volumes 07 and 08 and is not available to anybody, and the man who trades on the Open Hand board is thirty-nine. And *man of about thirty* is barred by a rule about a reader and not about an instrument: it stands at one occurrence in one file, the published definition exists to stop it being returned inside *man of about thirty-nine*, and a figure a person cannot see on a page quickly is a figure a person will see wrong on a page quickly.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses the convenience of a measured pool it can draw from, and gains a rule that is harder and better: a new person's descriptor must be measured at zero in the last two volumes *and* published with its four hundred-file figure beside it, and the set it comes from is the forty-seven, and the set is published in full so that the next phase inherits the reason and not only the rule.**

**AND WHERE ADRIAN VALE IS, WHICH WAS THE CLOSE'S FOURTH QUESTION AND IS THIS PHASE'S TO SET. HE IS NAMED IN NINE OF THE FIFTY CHAPTERS — 0402, 0405, 0414, 0422, 0428, 0432, 0436, 0441 and 0447 — AND IN NO CHAPTER IN ANY FORM IN THE OTHER FORTY-ONE, AND IN EIGHT OF THE NINE HE IS IN A ROOM READING A PAGE. Chapter 0436 IS THE NINTH AND IT IS THE CHAPTER IN WHICH HE READS NOTHING, and the close's requirement is met.** The number is nine against Volume 08's seven, and the reason is named: **a volume about a column that nobody fills has eight rooms in which there is a page and one morning on which there is not, and a man who is only ever on a page is a man this book has been using as a piece of furniture.**

**And the shape of that one chapter matters more than the fact of it. The rule that produced seven was that he is named where he reads a page and in no chapter where people decline to ask him, and the close found the rule to be a rule and not a law. Chapter 0436 is at about the seventh hour of a morning on the boards of a salt wharf at the bottom of this city, with a stack higher than a man, a strip of clear board about as long as a cart, a tally-board and a knife, and nothing on any of them that anybody has to fill in that morning. Nobody asks him anything, nobody declines to ask him anything, nobody is rude to him and nobody is waiting for him to be useful. He works something out in it and the chapter does not say he was right. THE BEND IS NOT A REFUSAL, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE DISCIPLINE: a chapter may put him somewhere and let him watch, and a chapter may let him work something out and must then not say he was right. No chapter may be opened, moved, retitled or reweighted to make room for it, and a batch may not add a tenth chapter to the list.**

**What that costs, plainly. The series keeps a man who is not the reason this book exists on a page nine times in fifty, one more than last time, and it pays for it with a chapter in which he is on a page and has nothing to do, which is the first chapter in four volumes in which the protagonist is present and idle. And it is nine, not fifteen, and the reason is that the instrument that put him in rooms has been spent.**

**AND THE SEVEN DEBTS INHERITED, PLUS THE FIGURES, AND ALL CARRIED AND NONE REPAIRED, because every one of them belongs to a repair pass and not to an outline.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290; `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149`, which says *sixteen* where 342 − 341 = 1, with the string to assert and the replacement published; a thirty-nine-word run across the 0254 and 0262 boundary; the unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47`; *about two hundred rooms* on five people and a woman, where the state layer once said zero; three sentences in Chapters 0364 and 0396 that put the shut road above this city; two figures in `bible/power-system.md` §50 item 9 that are Batch 0005's ten files. **All eight are at `outline/volume-09.md` §16 at their length and none was taken and none was repaired.**

**AND THE NINTH DEBT, WHICH THIS PHASE FOUND, AND IT IS THE MOST EXPENSIVE CLASS OF FAULT IN THIS REPOSITORY, BECAUSE IT IS A FALSE ZERO ABOUT A HELD STRING. THE SIX-WORD REFUSAL SPOKEN BY THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT IN CHAPTER 0393 IS ON A PAGE ONCE AND IS ALSO REPRODUCED VERBATIM, INSIDE BOLD MARKS, AT `bible/power-system.md:1684`, WHICH IS §50, THE PERMANENT WITNESS SECTION WRITTEN BY VOLUME 08'S BATCH 0005, AND IT IS ALSO PRINTED TWICE IN THIS REPOSITORY'S OWN VOLUME 08 PLAN AT `outline/volume-08.md:208`, WHICH NO MEASUREMENT NAMED UNTIL THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS ON THE VOLUME 09 CLOSE RE-RAN IT.** `state/volume-08-close.md` §4 and `reviews/volume-08/close.md` §6 both say it is nowhere else in this repository, and both statements are false, and the reason the check missed it is worth naming: **the run described in the close's review extracted the spans from Chapter 0357 and searched, and the six words live in a different chapter, and a hold measured from one chapter does not cover the other.** This phase ran the same instrument on both chapters. The words are not printed in this file and were not printed in `outline/volume-09.md` and are not printed in the batch cards or in the prompt this phase creates. **The repair belongs to a Volume 08 review repair pass, because §50 is a batch's permanent witness and a phase that quietly rewrites a witness to make a state file true is doing the thing this repository exists to catch.** The seven-word answer in Chapter 0357 is clean: all six of its bolded turns are in one file each, and that file is the chapter it is on the page of.

**AND THE TWO FIGURES THIS PHASE'S OWN INSTRUMENT DISAGREES WITH, published beside each other and neither deleted.** The four vocabulary rows at `outline/volume-09.md` §19.2, as above. And *about two hundred rooms* on Volume 08's fifty files: the close published ten occurrences in nine chapters and this phase's instrument returns ten occurrences in six files, 0352, 0360, 0367, 0369, 0374 and 0375, **so the occurrence count agrees and the chapter count does not, which means one chapter in the close's list carries the figure in a form this search does not reach. Settling it means attributing a figure to one of five people across nine pages of a finished volume, and that is a repair and not an outline's, so it is carried.** The rule that binds Volume 09 is decided and is not the attribution: **the figure is not available to a new person, no chapter of Volume 09 may give it to anybody, and the descriptor map for the volume records that five people and a woman already hold it.**

**AND WHAT THIS PHASE COULD NOT DECIDE, which is the same answer the last three phases gave and it is the answer that has now protected this book four times. The Bare Month does not end inside Volume 09, and this phase did not decide how long it is and cannot.** A volume that ended it on a chapter boundary would print the end of a month whose length a notary of fifty refused on a page, in the third volume to inherit that refusal, and it would have to do it in one of two ways: by a sentence in a state file, which is the thing this repository has been repairing for four volumes, or by a chapter that guesses, which `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 2 forbids. **So Volume 09 writes the eighty-sixth to the hundred and thirty-fifth day of the Bare Month and stops, and a seventh form exists the moment the end of it is on a page, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused before she does it and then declines to say how long the new month is.** This phase also decided not to amend §51, which it was permitted to do in one section, and did not take that section: the §33B contradiction at item 8a is routed to whichever phase next writes §33B and this phase did not write §33B.

**AND THE DAY MAP, because a weekday on a page outranks a weekday in a plan and the prompt for this phase required it to say which of the two it did.** **It was built from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else, then validated by recomputing days 301 to 350 and reproducing `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 4's table and days 351 to 400 and reproducing §33B's table, at ZERO MISMATCHS on all one hundred rows, and neither table was consulted until after the run was built.** §51's own fifty rows for days 401 to 450 were then checked row by row on chapter-equals-day, on weekday and on the Bare-Month ordinal being day less 315, and they agree at ZERO MISMATCHS on all fifty. **Day 401 is a Wednesday because day 400 is a Tuesday. Day 450 is a Wednesday because 450 − 401 = 49 = 7 × 7, and a day seven multiples of seven after another is the same weekday. Both ends are Wednesdays and no chapter may derive a middle day from either of them.** The midpoint is day 425, a Saturday, which is the nearer of the two middle days of a fifty-day range and may not be called the midpoint, because a fifty-day range has two middle days and a midpoint of 425.5. No day moved and no figure in §51 was corrected, because none of them is wrong.

**AND THE NEXT PHASE, BY PATH: `workspace/volume-09/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, and it is a batch and not an outline. It writes Chapters 0401 to 0410, days 401 to 410, from a Wednesday to a Friday, and it writes no decision and settles nothing, and the decisions are the nine in this block and the plan is `outline/volume-09.md` and its cards are in `outline/batches/volume-09-batch-0001.md`.**

---

## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 10 — taken by the Volume 09 close phase, in its own words

**Four decisions were open when Volume 09 was closed and none of them was decided by any batch, because a batch is not allowed to decide. The Volume 06 outline phase took four. The Volume 07 outline phase took four. The Volume 07 close phase took four. The Volume 08 outline phase took four more. The Volume 08 close phase took four. The Volume 09 outline phase took five more. This close phase is the seventh phase in this repository that has been permitted to decide, and it took four more. They are written here, in the series outline, so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost. Nothing in this file moved that was not named below, the ending did not move, no new final enemy was introduced, and the antagonist ladder and the relationship milestones are untouched. This block sits after the Volume 09 outline block and at the foot of this file, which is where the Volume 09 outline block sits, and a reader who wants the series and not the argument should skip all six blocks. There is no Volume 10 entry in the volume list that this phase wrote or adopted, and the one that is already there is named in the second of these four decisions and was not touched.**

**AND THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK IS NOT, repeated from the close record and repeated here because a decision block is a page a writer reads and this file is the second such page: the name of Volume 09 is a man's answer spoken once in a room in daylight with a date on the door in Chapter 0428, and it is on that page and in no file, and this block does not have it, and the calendar section this phase wrote in `bible/power-system.md` does not have it, and the five blocks it appended to the state layer do not have it, and the prompt it wrote does not have it. The volume's one rule, the panel of day 438, is in the same position and is held by the same rule. So are the answer spoken in Chapter 0357, the refusal spoken in Chapter 0393, the six words reproduced at `bible/power-system.md:1684` and in the Volume 08 plan, and the day-154 sentence. None of them is printed here and none of them is paraphrased here and no sentence here certifies that any of them is in no file, because `state/volume-09-close.md` §7.8 publishes the measurement that found one record asserting exactly that and being wrong.**

**DECISION ONE — THE VOICE. CONFIRMED, EIGHT TIMES NOW, AND IT CARRIES THREE THINGS THIS VOLUME FOUND, ALL THREE OF THEM ABOUT INSTRUMENTS AND NOT ABOUT PROSE.**

**Volume 06's voice is this book's voice and it is confirmed for the rest of the run. Fifty-day volumes, one chapter to one day, about a page and a yard and a wage in salt. Nobody gets stronger. The antagonist is a method and not a form, and Volume 09 proved the third half of that in a room with a date on the door: the method is a page that says where a line runs, put on a table, read out, and answered, and no document in the volume is defeated and nothing is solved. The only instrument this book has that a return cannot be filled in from is a blank. There is no narrator frame, and the narrator may not say *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch* or *the reader*, and Volume 02's thirty-one uses of *this volume* are named as the one place in the manuscript where a narrator may say it and nowhere else. Measured on this volume's fifty files on the published definitions, **the share of words inside bold marks runs 5.79 to 22.82 with a mean of 13.83 unweighted on 56,696 words; the count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker is zero in all fifty; the exchange is 123 plain speeches and 81 answered; the longest bolded turn in the fifty is eighty-nine words and no turn in the fifty is over ninety and none is over the hard ceiling of ninety-five; and there is one panel in fifty chapters.** The mean is a consequence and not a plan, no volume may read its own mean as a quality figure, and no review may certify or fail a volume on one. The range of fifty-day volumes is from here and is not a rule about the past: Volume 04 is the recorded exception and is not reopened, and a volume after that which wants a double day has to argue for it in its own outline against a rule that is now the default.**

**What Volume 09 adds is three rules, and they bind every batch after this line, and all three of them are about instruments and not about prose, which is the single most useful thing this volume has for the writer who comes after it.**

1. **A REPAIR PASS CAN REBUILD FIFTY SENTENCES WITHOUT TOUCHING FIFTY FACTS, AND THE TEST THAT IT HAS NOT IS NOT A WORD COUNT.** Batch 0005's ten files as that batch left them stood at a batch mean of fifty-three words to the sentence, per-chapter means from sixty-seven to a hundred and eighty-six, and a longest single sentence of a hundred and thirty-six. The repair pass rebuilt all ten files out of the sentences they already had: **twenty-six words to the sentence, per-chapter means twenty-two to thirty-three, a longest sentence of seventy-nine, the same two hundred and sixty-nine words of *nobody* chains cut to thirty-two, and four per cent shorter and not thinner. It added no fact, no day, no count and no event.** **The test that it has not touched a fact is not a word count. It is that the day run, the counts, the object inventory, the cast placements and the prohibitions all re-assert against the strings afterwards**, and this close re-ran all five on the ten files as they stand and they all hold. **Joinery is measurable and facts are not, and that is why a batch of a fifty-chapter volume can be put back together without a reader being able to say what changed — and it is also why a repair pass that adds a fact is a rewrite and not a repair, and a rewrite of a volume that is planned, written and closed is not permitted to anybody.**
2. **A HABIT THAT FOUR WRITTEN WARNINGS DID NOT REMOVE IS A RULE'S JOB AND NOT A WARNING'S, AND THE WAY YOU KNOW IT IS A HABIT AND NOT A FAULT IS THAT IT HAS A COUNT.** Nine batches' worth of instruction ran against **a set of numbers that recurs: nineteen rates, sixteen posts, eleven withies, about four people, about nine people, about nine yards, nine steps, four hundred miles.** Measured word-bounded across the fifty files: **four hundred miles forty-four in twenty-eight files, nine steps a hundred and one in forty-one, about four people ninety-four in thirty-nine, about nine people forty-four in thirty-one, nineteen rates thirty-nine in twenty-one, sixteen posts eight in six, eleven withies sixteen in eleven, about nine yards seven in three.** **A set of numbers that recurs is a rhythm the reader learns whether or not the writer meant it, and a rhythm is not a fault and is not an instrument's business, and the only thing that can be done about it is a rule.** The form the rule takes: **a phrase that has stood in four hundred and fifty chapters is a decision about the world and not about a sentence, and a decision about the world belongs to a plan and not to a batch.**
3. **A FIGURE AT ZERO IN AN INSTRUMENT A WRITER EXPECTED TO BE NON-ZERO IS THE ONE TO LOOK AT TWICE, AND THE HABIT RUNS BOTH WAYS.** Two chapters of Batch 0004 and two of Batch 0005 came out of a first pass with **no marked turn in them at all**, the bolded instrument read them as a legitimate 0.00 and the exchange instrument read them as a legitimate zero, **and the only instrument that came back clean was the one nobody expected anything from — the count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker — while the two instruments everybody expected to be fine were the two carrying the fault.** The same shape runs the other way in this volume: **the System appeared once in fifty chapters, on day 438, the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across all fifty files, and the last three volumes run at roughly a third of Volume 01's words a chapter over a vocabulary of about nineteen hundred types.** **A zero in this repository is a fact about an instrument and not a fact about a chapter, in both directions, and the instrument that returns zero where a writer expected a number and the instrument that returns a number where a writer expected a fault are the same failure.** Two instruments that both return a number the writer expected are not two instruments, and a zero gets read twice.

**AND A FOURTH FINDING THAT IS NOT A RULE AND IS THE ONE A NEXT OUTLINE HAS TO ACT ON: a fifty-chapter volume can end with its last ten chapters in single sentences of up to a hundred and eighty-six words and every one of the seven checks in `outline/volume-09.md` §20 can pass, because not one of the seven counts a word to a sentence.** The sentence-length figures are the only measure in this repository that caught that fault, and a rule that is not in a check is a warning, and the second of the three rules above is what a warning costs.

**What it costs, said plainly and not softened. The series keeps its voice and gets three instrument rules it did not have. It loses the right to treat a mean as a result, a zero as a fact about a chapter, a warning as an instrument, a published count without its instrument as a measurement, a repair pass as a rewrite, and a recurring number as an accident. The only thing this decision takes away is the convenience of the person reading a file, and that convenience is the thing that has been costing this manuscript a review finding for five volumes.**

**AND WHAT THIS PHASE COULD NOT DECIDE, which is the same answer the last four close phases gave and it is the answer that has now protected this book five times. The Bare Month does not end inside Volume 10, and this phase did not decide how long it is and cannot.** A volume that ended it on a chapter boundary would print the end of a month whose length a notary of fifty refused on a page, in the fourth volume to inherit that refusal, and it would have to do it in one of two ways: by a sentence in a state file, which is the thing this repository has been repairing for five volumes, or by a chapter that guesses, which `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 2 forbids. **So Volume 10 writes the hundred and thirty-sixth to the hundred and eighty-fifth day of the Bare Month and stops, and a seventh form exists the moment the end of it is on a page, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused before she does it and then declines to say how long the new month is.** A calendar is not a decision an outline takes and a batch guesses. It is a decision a person makes in a room, and every form in this book is one, including the ones in the bible.

**DECISION TWO — THE LINE. TO BE AUDITED BEFORE IT IS ADOPTED, ON THE CHAPTER FILES, WITH THE INSTRUMENT PUBLISHED, AND THE CHEAPEST INHERITANCE IN THIS REPOSITORY IS PUBLISHED HERE SO THAT THE NEXT OUTLINE STARTS THERE AND DOES NOT MEASURE FROM SCRATCH.**

**The standing test is at `outline/volume-07.md` §19 and it is the one that matters: a line is made of words the manuscript has never used AND of things the manuscript has never seen, and a line that is only the first collides with a page that already means something else. Volume 07's incoming line was struck and replaced in one act by the Volume 07 close phase, four of its nouns collided, Volume 08's incoming line was struck by the Volume 08 close phase for the same reason, and the audit that catches them is the audit run here.** This close does not write a line, because a line is a plan and a close records a volume. What it does is measure, and it measured on the chapter files and not on a state file, because a line this phase does not audit is a line this repository has already paid for twice.

**THE INSTRUMENT, PUBLISHED BESIDE EVERY FIGURE: every figure is a word-bounded count, the token bounded by whitespace, punctuation or a line end, case-sensitive unless the figure says otherwise, taken over whole files and not line by line, and THE COUNT WAS TAKEN OVER 450 CHAPTER FILES, fifty in each of nine volumes, and 450 is the number and a writer who counts 400 has left a volume out.**

**AT ZERO ACROSS ALL 450 CHAPTER FILES, word-bounded and case-sensitive, and still available to a line, and this is the list the next outline should start from:** *Sundering*, *constitution*, *template*, *templates*, *charters*, *Redroot Homes*, *caravan*, *convoy*, *itinerary*, *waybill*, *Commonwealth*, *commonwealth*, *filler*, *march*, *Earthsiders*, *isekai*, *System*, *sixth month*, *of next month*, *of this new month*, *this chapter*, *in this batch*, *fences*, *boundaries*, *journeys*, *orchards*, *hundreds*, *tolls*, *warrants*, *levies*, *inquest*, *coroner*, *bailiff*, *reeve*, *ledgers*, *huts*, *labor*, *withe*, *byre*, *sow*, *cartwright*, *pinder*, *tithe*, *muster*, *wain*, *dray*, *sledge*, *kibble*, *clamp*, *marl*, *lath*, *paling*, *wattle*, *coppice*, *ferry*, *cwtch*. **Fifty-five at zero, and thirty-six of the fifty-five are the inherited list reproducing exactly, and nineteen are new to this block and were measured in this run. Note that *labor* is at zero and *labour* stands at fifty-eight in thirty-seven, and that spelling is this manuscript's spelling, and a line built on the American spelling of it is a line about a word this manuscript has never used — that has now been corrected in this repository four times and is corrected again here because a phase that inherits a corrected fact inherits the reason and not only the figure. Note also that *hurdle*, *stile* and *barge* are NOT at zero and were checked, at twenty-five in nine, eight in five and three in one, and a writer who assumed otherwise would collide with a page that already means something else.**

**AND THE LINE'S OWN LOAD-BEARING NOUNS ARE NOT IN THAT LIST, and that is the finding and it is the same finding that made Volume 08's line and Volume 09's line work, and it is published here so that the next phase inherits the reason and not only the rule.** *Man* 7,232 in 448, *thing* 5,401 in 449, *asked* 4,823 in 445, *day* 6,669 in 450, *hundred* 4,041 in 441, *person* 3,417 in 423, *page* 3,238 in 414, *hand* 2,494 in 432, *road* 2,161 in 371, *line* 1,823 in 333, *name* 1,810 in 357, *question* 1,556 in 349, *word* 1,280 in 369, *slate* 1,271 in 287, *board* 1,193 in 271, *form* 847 in 218, *column* 1,106 in 256, *ground* 838 in 277, *end* 1,021 in 330, *thing*, *side* 592 in 238, *mark* 481 in 171, *step* 461 in 168, *rope* 425 in 125, *yard* 2,307 in 215, *barrow* 514 in 132, *sheet* 969 in 221, *register* 346 in 140, *roll* 529 in 173, *notice* 234 in 130, *post* 265 in 102, *fence* 185 in 34, *empty* 330 in 141, *blank* 52 in 30, *edge* 293 in 150, *withy* 9 in 3, *withies* 21 in 12, *tin* 53 in 15. **A line built on a noun at zero is a line about a thing this world has never seen, and the volumes that did that are the volumes that produced a series outline one volume behind. A line built on a noun in the high column is a line about the oldest thing in this book.** *Withies* is the one thing in Volume 09's line that was nearly new and *withy* is now nine in three across four hundred and fifty chapters, **and that is where a newness actually lives in this book: not in a noun but in a count of a thing somebody else built, made by a man nobody sent, and counted by four people and no office.**

**AND SEVEN TOKENS THAT ARE NOT AT ZERO AND THAT A LINE IN VOLUME 10 MAY NOT TREAT AS IF THEY WERE, with the 450-file figures and the scope stated because the scope is the fault:** *passage* **11 in 11** and at zero in the last three volumes, *privilege* **45 in 41** and at zero in the last three volumes, *delegation* 17 in 4 and sixteen of them in Volumes 03 and 04, *quest* 2 in 2 and available and owned, *Ivenn* 46 in 26 and at zero in the last three volumes and his placement is a debt this block inherits and does not pay, *Crown* **329 in 104** and the lower-case *crown* 1 in 1, and *holder* **234 in 95** on a great many pages and defined by nobody. **A volume that prints the Crown's office and its page and its sheet is inside the *Crown* row and does not need that debt.**

**AND THE ONE COLLISION THE AUDIT CAN ALREADY SEE, which is the finding that decides the shape of the next line more than any count: the question is spent.** *What is a line for, on a page, when the only thing anybody will use it for is to move it* is on a page in Chapter 0445, unanswered, and a Volume 10 line that is that question again is Volume 09 with the nouns changed, exactly as `outline/volume-09.md` §18 forbids. **So the next line has to be built on the standing the unanswered question left and not on the question itself**, and the standing is published at `state/volume-09-close.md` §3: a sheet that came back with one of four lines in a place it did not go out in and nobody in this city is going to be asked about it; a register with a day in it and nothing under the day; a second slate nobody has picked up in fifty chapters; a man who speaks a name once and refuses a rule ten days later and is thanked for neither; a woman who walked a market on two feet asking four strangers and has stopped; a length of rope that has never been used; three barrows on a road nobody can be shown one person on; and a woman whose line is the one that moved and who is never going to be asked about it.

**AND THE ENTRY FOR VOLUME 10 THAT IS ALREADY IN THE VOLUME LIST, WHICH THIS PHASE DID NOT TOUCH AND DID NOT ADOPT, and says so plainly because a writer planning Volume 10 will open this file and find it there.** The volume list carries a Volume 10 entry with a title, a central pressure, a midpoint, a climax, a resolution and a question. **It is a plan line from before this voice was confirmed, and it says in its own words that a place-name in it was struck as a plan line by an earlier decision and that the line still says it, which is the same class of honest debt this file has carried four times and is the right way to carry it. It also describes a protagonist who learns, destroys, loses a memory and lets somebody escape with a fragment, and that is Volume 08's and Volume 09's antagonist ladder running backwards from a version of this book that was planned before the method was decided. This close did not audit it, did not adopt it, did not strike it and did not change it, because an entry in the volume list is a line and a line is the outline phase's to write and audit, and because a close record that plans is a plan and a plan is not a record. THE VOLUME 10 OUTLINE PHASE ADOPTS, STRIKES OR REPLACES IT IN ITS OWN WORDS, EXACTLY AS THE VOLUME 09 OUTLINE PHASE STRUCK AND REPLACED THE LINE IT INHERITED, AND IT PUBLISHES THE SAME INSTRUMENT AND THE SAME COUNT WHEN IT DOES.**

**DECISION THREE — THE CALENDAR. DAYS 451 TO 500, ONE CHAPTER TO ONE DAY, THE FORM UNCHANGED, THE BARE MONTH STILL HAS NO STATED LENGTH, AND IT DOES NOT END.**

**The Bare Month has no stated length, by a notary's refusal on a page in Chapter 0316, and it did not end inside Volume 09 and no chapter of that volume ended it. So the next volume begins on an unknown number of days into a month of no stated length, and that is settled deliberately and not left to happen.** Volume 09 was days 401 to 450 and the month ran from its eighty-sixth to its hundred and thirty-fifth, **and fifty chapters fitted inside one month of no known length without approaching an end, and that is the strongest evidence this repository has that the device works, and it is the reason a sixth close phase in a row can decline to give the month a length without anybody being able to say the protection is running out.**

**Volume 10 is days 451 to 500, one chapter to one day, no double day, and the form is *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day less 315, which is the sixth form and is the one named on a page in Chapter 0316 and in no other chapter and in no other file. Day 451 is the hundred and thirty-sixth day of the Bare Month and a Thursday, because day 450 is a Wednesday; day 500 is the hundred and eighty-fifth and a Thursday, because 500 − 451 = 49 = 7 × 7. All fifty rows are derived from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else, and they are at `bible/power-system.md` §57, which this phase wrote, and §57 is a NEW NUMBERED SECTION in the same numbered form as §51 and it amends nothing in §32, §33, §33A, §33B, §51 or any other section. No batch may make, amend or extend any of it.**

**AND THE DECISION INSIDE THE DECISION, and it is the same one the last four close phases took and for the same reason: THE BARE MONTH DOES NOT END INSIDE VOLUME 10, AND §57 GIVES IT NO LENGTH AND SAYS SO IN A SENTENCE.** A chapter of Volume 10 may write an ordinal for any day of it from the hundred and thirty-sixth to the hundred and eighty-fifth. **No chapter may write a day after the hundred and eighty-fifth, may not say the month is nearly over, may not say what it is a month of, may not convert a count into a span, may not print *a hundred and eighty-five days* or *five months* or *since the month began* as a figure, and may not say what the month does at its end, how many days it has, or whether it is long or short. The words *length*, *long* and *short* applied to the month stay at zero.** The only two instruments a chapter has for a date are a count of days of this flood and a day of the Bare Month with its own form, and the second is the cheapest date in this book and the only one nobody has to check twice.

**And the rule for whoever comes after, unchanged in substance and restated because it is the point of deciding it here: a seventh form exists the moment the end of the Bare Month is on a page, and it comes onto a page the same way the sixth one did — a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused by somebody before she does it, and who then declines to say how long the new month is and says why. The Short Month and the Bare Month were both made that way, in Chapter 0256 and in Chapter 0316, by the same notary of fifty, who named one and then named the other for the same reason. A calendar is not a decision an outline takes and a batch guesses. It is a decision a person makes in a room, and every form in this book is one, including the ones in the bible. If a later volume needs the seventh form and has not got the end of the Bare Month on a page, it writes inside the Bare Month and IT MAY NOT DECLARE THE MONTH OVER.**

**What it costs. The series gets a calendar it can plan fifty days against, and it pays for it with a seventh form that still does not exist: Volume 10 is a volume that begins on an unknown number of days into a month of no known length and ends on another one, and the protection that buys is that no volume can inherit an end by accident. A hundred and eighty-five days of a month of no stated length would be the longest stretch any volume in this series has spent inside one month, and the alternative was to give the month a length in a bible file, which is the fault this repository has been repairing for five volumes, or in a chapter that guesses, which §33A item 2 forbids.**

**DECISION FOUR — THE CAST, THE INSTRUMENT, THE POOL, AND HOW MANY ARE LEFT IN IT.**

**No name and no place arrives by narration in this book. It is checked for a room, in daylight, with a date on it, by a person. Volume 06's state layer broke it by naming a gatekeeper who was on no page and a review caught it, and the name is in the repository only as the record of the strike. The instrument is the oldest one in the book and it is unchanged: a document arrives and is read out, and the person who reads it out says out loud what it is for before anybody asks, and the four days of notice is what brings people to a room, and a notice on any other day is a three-day instrument and a three-day instrument does not fail, it arrives warm. And there is no eighth notice in this flood, and Volume 10 sends none either, and the page that says so is `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0389.md:35`.**

**THE POOL, measured on this close's own instrument and published here so that the next phase inherits the reason and not only the rule. Definition: every occurrence at sentence level of the word *man*, *woman*, *person*, *girl* or *boy*, followed by *of*, *about* and a spelled-out number — a tens word optionally hyphenated with a units word, or a teen, or a units word, or the hundred forms — bounded by whitespace, punctuation or a line end, on a file with the emphasis marks removed and the whitespace collapsed, so that *man of about thirty* is not returned inside *man of about thirty-nine*. Files: the 450 chapter files.** The instrument was validated before it was used, by reproducing `outline/volume-09.md` §20.8's one new descriptor at three occurrences in one file.

**Volume 09's map is FIFTEEN distinct descriptors standing across its fifty files, against nineteen in Volume 08's fifty and twenty-nine in Volume 07's fifty, and that is the smallest map in this series: *man of about thirty-four* 74 in 33 files, *man of about thirty-nine* 48 in 26, *woman of about twenty-nine* 37 in 29, *man of about fifty-seven* 35 in 23, *man of about thirty-eight* 22 in 7, *man of about sixty* 15 in 5, *man of about twenty-seven* 11 in 3, *man of about thirty-one* 5 in 4, *man of about forty-one* 5 in 4, *man of about fifty-nine* 5 in 2, *woman of about fifty-two* 4 in 2, *woman of about fifty-four* 3 in 1, *woman of about sixty-nine* 2 in 1, *man of about twenty-five* 1 in 1, *man of about thirty-six* 1 in 1. A map that shrinks is a map that is measuring a volume with fewer people in it, and this volume has one new person in it and no notices in it and an instrument that is a sheet read out to the nine who were already in the room.**

**AND HOW MUCH OF THE POOL IS LEFT, which is the finding this close took and it is a decision and not a measurement. Fifty-seven descriptors stand at zero in BOTH Volume 08's fifty files and Volume 09's fifty files, and that is the raw set a new person may be drawn from, against a published set of forty-seven two volumes ago. From those fifty-seven, subtract: the two PROTECTED, *man of about thirty-seven* at 6 in 1 file and *girl of about seventeen* at 2 in 2 files; the three barred by the reader rule at `outline/volume-09.md` §7.2, *man of about thirty* at 1 in 1, *woman of about thirty* at 27 in 14 and *man of about forty* at 25 in 6, because a figure a person cannot see on a page quickly is a figure a person will see wrong on a page quickly; the ones in the raw set that already have a page — *man of about forty-four* 162 in 85, *woman of about twenty-four* 112 in 52, *man of about thirty-two* 61 in 35, *man of about twenty-nine* 57 in 34, *woman of about thirty-three* 34 in 24, *woman of about thirty-nine* 23 in 11, *woman of about thirty-eight* 19 in 15, *woman of about thirty-six* 13 in 10, *boy of about eleven* 13 in 6, *woman of about twenty-eight* 11 in 5, *woman of about sixty* 8 in 7, *man of about sixty-three* 6 in 2 and *man of about sixty-four* 4 in 1, which are both in Volume 09, *man of about thirty-three* 2 in 1, *person of about twenty-one* 2 in 1 and *man of about twenty-one* 1 in 1; and the one Volume 09 took, *woman of about fifty-four*, which is not in the fifty-seven because it now stands in Volume 09. **WHAT IS LEFT IS THIRTY-SIX, published in full at `state/volume-09-close.md` §7.3 and in the instrument paragraph above, and every one of the survivors is a descriptor with a low four-hundred-and-fifty-file figure and no page.** THAT IS THE ANSWER TO WHAT HAPPENS TO THE NEXT VOLUME THAT HAS TO FIND ONE, AND IT IS THIS: the pool has roughly halved in two volumes, it is not going to be replenished from inside this manuscript at this rate, and a phase that runs out of it will be forced to invent a person, which is the one thing this repository's cast rules exist to prevent. SO THE RULE THIS PHASE DECIDES IS THAT A NEW PERSON'S DESCRIPTOR MUST BE MEASURED AT ZERO IN THE LAST TWO VOLUMES AND PUBLISHED WITH ITS FOUR-HUNDRED-AND-FIFTY-FILE FIGURE BESIDE IT, AND THAT A PHASE WHICH FINDS ITSELF CHOOSING FROM A SET OF FEWER THAN TWELVE MUST SAY SO IN ITS OWN OUTLINE AND NAME THE DESCRIPTOR IT IS USING, RATHER THAN TAKING ONE QUIETLY. And a descriptor at zero in one volume and enormous in the six before it is a descriptor with a person attached to it that no page has ever named, and that is the exact shape of the fault the map exists to catch.**

**AND THE TWO DESCRIPTOR DEBTS, both of which this phase is CARRYING, and says so, because a close record that carries a debt silently is a record nobody can check.** *(1) The figure **about two hundred rooms** stands at ten occurrences in six files of Volume 08 — 0352 three, 0360 one, 0367 three, 0369 one, 0374 one, 0375 one — where the state layer once said zero, and the attribution is unpaid. **The rule is settled and binds whatever comes next: the figure is not available to a new person, no chapter of Volume 10 may give it to anybody, and the descriptor map records that at least five people and a woman already hold it.** *(2) **One room count IS printed in Volume 09, and four of its five batches published that none was.** The rule stands — no chapter prints a room count for anybody, in narration or in a mouth, on a list of cardinals or on a list that includes *dozen*, and the standing reason is a figure that goes down from ten to nine over three days because it is a subtraction taken across the wrong boundary — **and `chapters/volume-09/chapter-0416.md:11` prints one, in narration, a dozen rooms, and the rule named that list. This close carries it, publishes the line, and routes it to a Volume 09 review repair pass. A close phase that quietly takes a routed item to save itself a line is how nineteen quotation marks became a five-volume debt.**

**AND WHERE ADRIAN VALE IS, WHICH IS THE NEXT OUTLINE'S TO SET AND THIS PHASE DOES NOT SET IT. He is named in NINE of Volume 09's fifty chapters — 0402, 0405, 0414, 0422, 0428, 0432, 0436, 0441 and 0447 — and in no chapter in any form in the other forty-one, and the nine are the plan's own list and not a batch's doing. In eight of the nine he is in a room reading a page. Chapter 0436 is the ninth and it is the one morning on which he reads nothing, and nobody asks him anything and nobody declines to ask him anything and nobody is waiting for him to be useful, and the chapter does not say he was right. THE FINDING THIS PHASE CONFIRMS AND THE REVIEW RAISED IS THIS: THE RULE THAT PRODUCED SEVEN IN VOLUME 08 AND NINE IN VOLUME 09 IS NOW TWO VOLUMES OLD AND IT HAS COST THIS SERIES ITS PROTAGONIST ACROSS TWO VOLUMES, and this is a real finding about a rule and not an aesthetic preference, and it belongs in the brief of whatever plans the next one. Nine against seven is one more and the reason is named: a volume about a column nobody fills has eight rooms in which there is a page and one morning on which there is not, and a man who is only ever on a page is a man this book has been using as a piece of furniture. THE REMEDY IS A DECISION AND NOT A REPAIR, no chapter of Volume 09 may be opened or moved to make room for one, and no chapter of Volume 10 may get him stronger, perform a working, open a threshold, name the other world, or be offered a workway he accepts.**

**AND THE SEVEN DEBTS INHERITED PLUS THE TWO VOLUME 09 ADDED, ALL CARRIED AND NONE REPAIRED, because every one of them belongs to a repair pass and not to a writer. Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290; `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149`, which says *sixteen* where 342 − 341 = 1, with the string to assert and the replacement published; a thirty-nine-word run across the 0254 and 0262 boundary; the unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47`; *about two hundred rooms*; three sentences in Chapters 0364 and 0396 that put the shut road above this city; two figures in `bible/power-system.md` §50 item 9 that are Batch 0005's ten files; the ninth, which is not a debt about a page: the six-word refusal spoken in Chapter 0393 is reproduced verbatim inside bold marks at `bible/power-system.md:1684`, which is a permanent witness section, and in the Volume 08 plan at `outline/volume-08.md:208`, and two records assert an absence that is false. Plus two new: the one room count at `chapter-0416.md:11`, and a second instance of *claim* in Volume 09 at `chapter-0404.md` where the volume's own plan gave the word as the one the answer must not contain — published, and not treated as a fault, because a chapter that says a thing out loud in a mouth is not a chapter that names the word as a volume's noun. ALL TEN ARE AT `state/volume-09-close.md` §7.7 AT THEIR LENGTH WITH THE LINE NUMBERS, AND NONE WAS TAKEN AND NONE WAS REPAIRED.**

**AND THE OFF-BY-ONE THAT WAS CARRIED FOR TWO BATCHES AND WAS NOT ONE, corrected here so that no phase inherits a blocker that is not real. `bible/power-system.md` §51 item 9 does not print a wrong figure and does not disagree with anything: it states the rule and the figure in one clause and leaves one input of the rule open on purpose — three hundred and forty-nine at day 450 LESS ONE FOR EVERY DAY INSIDE THE RANGE ON WHICH THE WALL SAYS SOMETHING, and Volume 09 has one panel to spend and has not decided its day. The volume spent that panel on day 438, which closes the input, and 349 − 1 is THREE HUNDRED AND FORTY-EIGHT, which is the figure this repository publishes at day 450 and which a chain carried independently from 310 at day 411 also reaches. A formula with an input still open is not a printed answer, and four records read the input as an answer and each of them then built itself a permanent blocker to protect a section it had no need to touch. §51 IS UNAMENDED, IT IS WHOLE, IT NEEDS NO AMENDMENT, AND NOTHING IS OWED BY THE NEXT PHASE.**

**AND THE TWO CONTROLLER FAULTS, NAMED AND NOT WORKED AROUND, because they are not this phase's to touch and a phase that finds a fault in a controller and quietly works around it has made the controller worse. `state/phase-ledger.json` has never moved past `phase-000-bootstrap` and `status: planned` after fifty-odd completed phases, and `scripts/novel_runner.sh` derives its phase id with `basename`, so all nine volumes' `batch-0005` phases share one log file and one branch name and have been overwriting each other. Both files are in the list this agent may not edit, neither was opened, and both are named for the owner of the workflow.**

**AND THE ONE GAP IN THIS VOLUME'S OWN PAPERWORK THAT A CLOSE OWNS AND DID NOT FIX. `outline/batches/` holds six files and Volume 09 has one. `outline/batches/volume-09-batch-0002.md`, `-0003.md`, `-0004.md` and `-0005.md` do not exist, four of this volume's five batches were told to read one of them before reading anything else, and all four were written from `outline/volume-09.md` alone. It was disclosed four times and a fifth disclosure was not a remedy. This close did not write the four files, and the reason is worth more than the fix: a card file written after the ten chapters are on disk is a reconstruction, and four of them would put a second account of four finished batches into this repository beside the first and leave a writer with two plans and fifty finished chapters. What it cost is published: the pressure-rotation table listed one chapter in two rows and omitted another from every row so the counts summed to fifty over forty-nine chapters, and the object inventory's chapter column named four chapters the written chapters do not do and missed one they do, and both went unrepaired through two batches before a review found them. A plan that is only ever read whole gets its bookkeeping wrong and nobody notices for two volumes. THE REMEDY IS STRUCTURAL AND IT IS MADE IN THE ONE PLACE A CLOSE IS ALLOWED TO MAKE IT: the Volume 10 outline prompt requires its own first batch's card file to exist before that batch runs, and a batch which finds its card file missing is to stop and say so rather than write from the outline alone.**

**AND THE NEXT PHASE, BY PATH: `workspace/volume-10/outline/PROMPT.md`, and it is a VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE and not a batch. It writes `outline/volume-10.md`, Volume 10's first batch's cards at `outline/batches/volume-10-batch-0001.md`, and no prose, and it decides the line, the cast, the shape of the next volume's protagonist, and it audits the line before it adopts it, publishes the instrument with every figure, and writes no chapter. It amends nothing in `bible/power-system.md`, and it may not amend §51 or §57, and it may not make a seventh form, and it may not move a day, and it may not write a card file for any batch but its own first one, and it may not print any of the six held things this block has named and not printed.**

## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 10 — ADDED AND AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 10 OUTLINE PHASE, in its own words

**The four decisions above were taken by the Volume 09 close phase and this phase inherited all four without moving any of them. The Volume 10 line, the shape of the protagonist, the calendar, the cast and the instrument are exactly as that phase wrote them except where this block says in its own words that it changed them, and the four things it changed are the four this phase is permitted to change. This phase is the eighth phase in this repository that has been permitted to decide, and it took five more, and the date is the one the harness records for `workspace/volume-10/outline`. They are written here, in the series outline, so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost. Nothing else in this file moved, the ending did not move, no new final enemy was introduced, and the antagonist ladder and the relationship milestones are untouched. This block sits at the foot of this file, which is where the Volume 09 outline block sits, and a reader who wants the series and not the argument should skip all seven blocks.**

**AND THE FOUR THINGS THIS PHASE DID ARE ON DISK AND NAMED HERE, because the phase that wrote the four decisions above was caught by a review describing work it had not done, and a block that claims an act is worth nothing to the writer who comes after it unless it was.** `outline/volume-10.md`; `outline/batches/volume-10-batch-0001.md`, ten cards, **written before the outline and never reconstructed**; this block, including the strike of the volume-list entry; and the four state files repointed at `workspace/volume-10/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, which is written. **No chapter was written. No day was moved. `outline/ending.md` was not opened. `bible/power-system.md` was not edited, and §51 and §57 are unchanged, and §57 is the strongest single fact about this volume's calendar layer: the outline phase that was permitted to amend it chose not to, and the fifty-row day table and the twenty round figures and the twelve non-round traps and the interval anchors in it are confirmed by this phase's own independent run and not corrected by it.**

**AND THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK IS NOT, repeated from the close record and repeated here because a decision block is a page a writer reads and this file is the second such page: the name of Volume 09 is a man's answer spoken once in a room in daylight with a date on the door in Chapter 0428, and it is on that page and in no file this phase wrote, and the volume's one rule, the panel of day 438, is in the same position, and so are the answer spoken in Chapter 0357, the refusal spoken in Chapter 0393, the six words reproduced at `bible/power-system.md:1684` and in the Volume 08 plan at `outline/volume-08.md:208`, and the day-154 sentence. None of them is printed here and none of them is paraphrased here and no sentence here certifies that any of them is in no file, because `state/volume-09-close.md` §7.8 publishes the measurement that found one record asserting exactly that and being wrong, and because a plan file is not a page and a permanent witness section is not a page either. THE HOLD IS ON THIS PHASE'S OWN FILES AND NOT ON WHERE A STRING STANDS.**

**DECISION FIVE — THE LINE, AND THE ENTRY THAT WAS STANDING IN THE VOLUME LIST IS STRUCK, AND THE AUDIT THAT STRUCK IT IS PUBLISHED WITH IT.** The standing test is at `outline/volume-07.md` §19: a line is made of words the manuscript has never used AND of things the manuscript has never seen, and a line that is only the first collides with a page that already means something else.

**The entry in the volume list is struck, in the same act and the same words as the Volume 05, 07, 08 and 09 lines were struck, and the title in that entry is struck with it.** It is a plan line from before this voice was confirmed; it describes a party travelling beneath a place to find a source, a protagonist who learns, destroys, loses a memory and lets somebody escape with a fragment, an Earthside public reveal and a joint compact, and a question about who will use public truth to build a war. **Four reasons and the four are the finding.** Its instrument is a journey, and this manuscript has had no journey in three volumes and has a standing prohibition on one. It spends a memory, destroys a document and moves an antagonist, and nobody in this series gets stronger, no document is defeated and a person does not escape with a piece of anything. It introduces an antagonist question, and this series' antagonist is a method and not a form and a method does not get a question, it gets a room, a date and a cost. And **it carried a known fault in its own words for a volume — a place-name an earlier decision had struck, flagged in the entry itself — and a plan line that carries its own known fault is a plan whose other sentences cannot be trusted, so the fix is the strike and not another note.** Full text of the strike at `outline/volume-10.md` §19.4 and three sentences are kept from it and named there.

**What replaces it, in one sentence: *a board in a market that has stood unwritten for the whole of this flood is the only instrument in this city that has not lied to anybody, and a figure has come up the road that this city cannot check, and there is nothing in four hundred miles to put two numbers about the same thing side by side except that board, and the man who washes it every morning has decided not to wash the wall above it, and a room is going to write on it.*** **AND THE HONEST HALF OF THE TEST IS PUBLISHED RATHER THAN LEFT FOR A REVIEWER, and it is the same result that made Volume 08's line and Volume 09's line work and the prompt for this phase required it in these words: every load-bearing noun of this line is in the high column of this manuscript's own vocabulary and not one of them is at zero.** *Board* 1,193 in 271 files, *chalk* 917 in 286, *count* 550 in 206, *figure* 485 in 180, *household* 519 in 142, *house* 493 in 151, *day* 6,669 in 450, *person* 3,417 in 423, *page* 3,238 in 414. **The second half of the test is therefore carried by three things this manuscript has never had on a page, named at `outline/volume-10.md` §19.5 so that a writer can check them and a reviewer can fail them: a page with ONE place under a heading instead of four; two figures about the same kind of thing, kept in two places, in two different units, that cannot be turned into one another; and a surface that cannot be cleared without losing a record and cannot be added to without producing two records.** And the newness of the words is one word wide and it is the ordinaryest word in the language: **the word for the unit is at zero across all four hundred and fifty chapter files**, with eighteen other ordinary words beside it at zero, and the twenty are published in full so that a later phase inherits the reason and not only the rule.

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses a journey, a place-name, a memory, a destruction, an escape, an antagonist beat, a public reveal and a relationship milestone, none of which was owed, and it gets the least dramatic volume in the run so far: a figure comes up a road, a room decides faster than it should, a figure goes on a wall in chalk beside an old one, a man refuses a rule in about four seconds, and the figure and the old one are still there on the last page.** The antagonist ladder, the relationship milestones and `outline/ending.md` are untouched and no new final enemy is introduced.

**DECISION SIX — THE VOLUME'S NAME IS SPOKEN ON DAY 486 BY THE MAN WHO WASHES THE BOARD, AND THIS PHASE DOES NOT WRITE IT DOWN, AND IT ARRANGED EVERYTHING EXCEPT IT.** The only established way a volume in this series gets a name is a person saying it out loud in a room in daylight with a date on the door, and an outline that chooses one is choosing a title. **So the volume's name is not in this file and is not in `outline/volume-10.md` and is not in the ten cards and is not in the prompt this phase creates, and this block does not restate the standing entry's title either, because the standing entry is struck and a strike is not a description.** What is decided: the day, the room, the hour, the person asked — the man of about twenty-seven who has washed that board every morning since the seventh week of this flood and has never been asked what he is doing to it — the person who asks — the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register — the shape, the length, the words it may not contain, the words it is barred from because they are the volume's own nouns, and the consequence, which is that the same man refuses this volume's one rule four days later in the same room and what it costs him is the reason he has been keeping that board clean. Arrangement at `outline/volume-10.md` §6.5.

**What it costs, plainly. Nothing except the risk that a writer arrives at Chapter 0486 with nothing, and the risk is smaller than it looks: he has thirty-five days and a room, and the answer this block commits to in advance is that he says less than the plan expected and the volume is better for it.**

**DECISION SEVEN — THE POOL OF DESCRIPTORS A NEW PERSON MAY BE DRAWN FROM DOES NOT EXIST, MEASURED ON ALL FOUR HUNDRED AND FIFTY CHAPTER FILES, AND VOLUME 10 ADDS NO NEW PERSON AND INVENTS NOTHING.** The close published a pool of thirty-six and a subtraction that gives thirty-six. **This phase ran the same instrument over all 450 chapter files and then checked the survivors against the whole manuscript instead of against the last two volumes, and every one of the thirty-seven is standing on a page in a finished volume — thirty-seven of thirty-seven — and the clean pool is zero.** A descriptor at zero in two consecutive volumes of a four-hundred-and-fifty-chapter manuscript is not unused; it is between uses, and a two-volume window is the instrument's blind spot and not a filter.

**AND THE ONE THAT MATTERS IS NAMED, because a measurement nobody acts on is a rumour.** The one person the last volume's plan and close both published as **its single new person** stands at six occurrences in two files across the manuscript, and three of them are in `chapters/volume-06/chapter-0294.md` at lines 15, 25 and 33, where she has had a sheet face up on a sill for four days, puts a sheet down on a step and goes back in, and stands on the step in the one place on that lane where the sun is not and says two sentences in her own mouth. **She was not new. Nothing on any page has ever joined her to the woman of Chapter 0420 and nothing on any page ever will, because a descriptor map counts occurrences and does not decide which occurrence is the same person.** This phase does not repair it, does not attribute it, and does not print a number against a person across two finished volumes: **that is a Volume 09 review repair pass and the standing rule that forbids a chapter merging two people of one descriptor is at `outline/volume-10.md` §16.14 and this is the case that made it necessary.**

**What is decided and binds: Volume 10 adds no new person and takes no descriptor for anybody; a descriptor is available to a new person only if it stands at three occurrences or fewer across all 450 chapter files, every one of those occurrences is a passing mention and not a scene, and it is at zero in each of the last three volumes' fifty; the crowded four are still not available to anybody; *woman of about fifty-four* is not available to anybody and is two people until a repair pass says otherwise; and the only person this volume puts on a page properly is a person who is already on one and who has no age and no descriptor on any page, put there by being asked, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door.** The next volume that needs a person has a problem and the problem is named so that the phase that meets it does not solve it by making one up: **a new person in this book will have to arrive without a number, and a writer who cannot see how to do that has to say so in its own outline.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses the convenience of a measured pool it can draw from, and the pool turns out never to have been one — the usable set has fallen from something in the fifties to thirty-seven to zero in three volumes and the real number was zero before any of those measurements, and no instrument in this repository was looking in the right place.** And a second thing is lost, and it is smaller: the volume that wanted a stranger in the room does not get one, and a chapter about a figure is a chapter about a room about nine people who were already in it.

**DECISION EIGHT — WHERE ADRIAN VALE IS, AND THE RULE THAT PRODUCED SEVEN AND THEN NINE IS STRUCK AND REPLACED BY A RULE INSTEAD OF A WARNING.** The close left this to the outline and said so. **He is named in fifteen of the fifty chapters — 0453, 0454, 0458, 0461, 0464, 0466, 0471, 0476, 0479, 0482, 0485, 0488, 0491, 0494 and 0496 — and in no chapter in any form in the other thirty-five, and the fifteen are three in each movement of ten and the whole list is published in the day map's own column so that a card cannot put him in a chapter the map does not have him in.** The rule that produced seven and nine is struck. **It selected for reading a page, and in eight of Volume 09's nine he was in a room reading a page, and the review of that volume's last batch found him furniture in both of its ten chapters, and a man who is only ever on a page is a piece of furniture.** The rule that replaces it is about his hands: **HE IS IN NO CHAPTER IN WHICH HE IS READING A PAGE. Not one of the fifteen, and the three chapters in this volume in which a page is read out, a decision is taken or a figure goes on a board in front of about nine people are 0455, 0475 and 0490 and he is in none of them.** Nine of the fifteen are at the board or in that market and six are at a wharf, on the flats, on a lane or in the weather. **In at least four of the fifteen — 0461, 0479, 0488 and 0494 — nobody asks him anything, nobody declines to ask him anything, nobody is waiting for him to be useful, and he is not reading anything, and a chapter may let him work something out and must then not say he was right. AND THE RULE THAT MAKES IT A RULE: A CARD MAY NOT PUT HIM IN A CHAPTER UNLESS THE CARD NAMES THE THING HIS HANDS ARE ON. No hands, no chapter.** And no chapter may be opened, moved, retitled or reweighted to make room for one of the fifteen and a batch may not add a sixteenth.

**What it costs, plainly, and it is the opposite cost from the last volume's and it is not better, only different. The series gets its protagonist back on a page in fifteen chapters of fifty for the first time since Volume 07, and it pays with a man who spends fifteen chapters washing boards, carrying slates, walking eleven miles of flats in weather and standing at a wall with his hands idle, and not one of those fifteen chapters contains anything he is qualified to do.** A man who is only ever on a page is furniture; a man who is only ever at a board is a different kind of furniture; **and the difference is that a reader can now see him not being useful, which this book has never put on a page.** **Nobody in this volume gets stronger, he performs no working, no threshold is opened, the other world is not named once, nobody offers him a workway and he asks for none, the words *passage* and *privilege* stay at zero, and he is aged nowhere.**

**DECISION NINE — THIS PHASE FOUND THREE THINGS ABOUT INSTRUMENTS, AND ALL THREE ARE THE MOST TRANSFERABLE THING IT HAS, AND NONE OF THEM IS ABOUT PROSE.** The close asked for figures with instruments and this phase has three.

1. **TWO PUBLISHED TABLES, ONE PUBLISHED DEFINITION, TWO INSTRUMENTS, AND BOTH INSTRUMENTS REPRODUCE THEIR OWN TABLE EXACTLY ON EVERY CELL OF IT.** The definition says a token is delimited by whitespace, punctuation or a line end and does not say what a hyphen and an apostrophe are; a hyphen is punctuation. On the reading that takes that sentence literally — letters only, hyphen and apostrophe both delimit — the thirty-seven-row vocabulary table in the fourth of the close's four decisions **reproduces at thirty-seven of thirty-seven occurrence figures and thirty-seven of thirty-seven file figures over all 450 chapter files, with no cell out by one in either direction.** On the reading that takes a hyphen and an apostrophe as continuing a word, the thirteen-row table in `outline/volume-09.md` §19.2 **reproduces at thirteen of thirteen over its 400 files.** Each table is exactly right. **The two tables were not taken with the same instrument, neither can see the other, and the sentence that produced both of them is one sentence. The tell is nameable: the rows the readings separate are the rows that take a hyphen or an apostrophe in this manuscript, and both tables have the same eight such rows in them and they come out the other way.** This phase adopts the literal reading for its own figures, prints both, and **does not amend `outline/volume-09.md` and does not delete a published figure, and a later phase that prefers the other reading has to publish the column it disagrees with rather than change a number quietly.**
