2. **A PUBLISHED ZERO THAT IS NOT ZERO IS THE SAME FAILURE AS A WRONG COUNT, AND THE HABIT RUNS BOTH WAYS, AND NOBODY HAS EVER RUN IT.** The close published a list of fifty-six ordinary words at zero across all 450 chapter files **for the express purpose of letting the next outline start there instead of measuring from scratch.** Re-measured on the files: **fifty-five of the fifty-six reproduce at exactly zero and one does not, and the one is *sledge*, at seven occurrences in two files of Volume 04.** It is not a rare word and not a technical one. **A writer handed that list and building a line out of it would have put a sledge in Chapter 0451 and been two pages into a finished volume before anybody caught it, and no instrument in this repository would have caught it, because the list is a state-layer figure and a check of numerals cannot see a state-layer figure.**
3. **A MEASUREMENT TAKEN ON A WINDOW IS A FACT ABOUT THE WINDOW.** Decision seven is the demonstration and it is in the paragraph above: the pool was built on a two-volume window and the window hid thirty-seven people. **The general form, and it binds every batch after this line: every figure a batch publishes about a vocabulary, a descriptor, a phrase or a habit is published over the whole manuscript and not over a window, and a figure published over a window says so in the same line and says what the window is.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series gains three rules and the cheapest of them is a rule about publishing a flag. It loses the right to quote a published figure without its reading, to trust a state-layer list of zeros, and to use any windowed measurement as a fact about the manuscript. The only thing this decision takes away is the convenience of the person reading a file, and that convenience is the thing that has been costing this manuscript a review finding for six volumes.**

**AND WHAT THIS PHASE COULD NOT DECIDE, which is the same answer the last five close phases gave and it is the answer that has now protected this book six times. The Bare Month does not end inside Volume 10, and this phase did not decide how long it is and cannot.** A volume that ended it on a chapter boundary would print the end of a month whose length a notary of fifty refused on a page, in the fifth volume to inherit that refusal, and it would have to do it in one of two ways: by a sentence in a state file, which is the fault this repository has been repairing for six volumes, or by a chapter that guesses, which `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 2 forbids. **So Volume 10 writes the hundred and thirty-sixth to the hundred and eighty-fifth day of the Bare Month and stops, and a seventh form exists the moment the end of it is on a page, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused before she does it and who then declines to say how long the new month is.** A calendar is not a decision an outline takes and a batch guesses. **It is a decision a person makes in a room, and every form in this book is one, including the ones in the bible.**

**AND THE DAY MAP, because a weekday on a page outranks a weekday in a plan and the prompt for this phase required it to say which of the two it did.** **It was built from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else, then validated by recomputing days 301 to 350 and reproducing `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 4's table, days 351 to 400 and reproducing §33B's table, and days 401 to 450 and reproducing §51 item 2's own table, at ZERO MISMATCHS on all one hundred and fifty rows, and neither table was consulted until after the run was built. §57's own fifty rows for days 451 to 500 were then checked row by row on chapter-equals-day, on weekday and on the Bare-Month ordinal being day less 315, and they agree at ZERO MISMATCHS on all fifty.** Day 451 is a Thursday because day 450 is a Wednesday; day 500 is a Thursday because 500 − 451 = 49 = 7 × 7. **Both ends are Thursdays and no chapter may derive a middle day from either of them, and that the two ends are the same weekday is arithmetic and not a symmetry anybody is allowed to use.** The midpoint is day 475, a Sunday, the hundred and sixtieth, which is the nearer of the two middle days of a fifty-day range and may not be called the midpoint, because a fifty-day range has two middle days and a midpoint of 475.5. No day moved and no figure in §51 or §57 was corrected, because none of them is wrong.

**AND THE NEXT PHASE, BY PATH: `workspace/volume-10/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, and it is a batch and not an outline. It writes Chapters 0451 to 0460, days 451 to 460, one chapter to one day, from a Thursday to a Saturday, and it writes no decision and settles nothing, and the decisions are the nine in this block and the plan is `outline/volume-10.md` and its cards are in `outline/batches/volume-10-batch-0001.md`, and that file exists, and a batch that finds its own card file missing is to stop and say so rather than write from the outline alone.**

---

## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 11 — taken by the Volume 10 close phase, in its own words

**Four decisions were open when Volume 10 was closed and none of them was decided by any batch, because a batch is not allowed to decide. The Volume 06 outline phase took four. The Volume 07 outline phase took four. The Volume 07 close phase took four. The Volume 08 outline phase took four more. The Volume 08 close phase took four. The Volume 09 outline phase took five more. The Volume 09 close phase took four. The Volume 10 outline phase took five more. This close phase is the ninth phase in this repository that has been permitted to decide, and it took four more. They are written here, in the series outline, so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost. Nothing in this file moved that was not named below, the ending did not move, no new final enemy was introduced, and the antagonist ladder and the relationship milestones are untouched. This block sits after the Volume 10 outline block and at the foot of this file, which is where the Volume 10 outline block sits, and a reader who wants the series and not the argument should skip all of the blocks headed *DECISIONS OF RECORD*, and THE COUNT IS PUBLISHED BESIDE THE INSTRUCTION BECAUSE THE FIRST EDITION OF THIS SENTENCE SAID EIGHT AND WAS OFF BY ONE, IT WAS CORRECTED TO NINE BY THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS, AND IT WAS CORRECTED TO TEN BY THE VOLUME 11 OUTLINE PHASE, AND IT IS NOW ELEVEN BECAUSE THE VOLUME 12 OUTLINE PHASE ADDED THE ELEVENTH AT THE FOOT OF THIS FILE, and the clause that gave ten is preserved in its own words beneath this one rather than left for a reader to find. THE COUNT IS PUBLISHED HERE FOR THE THIRD TIME RATHER THAN LEFT FOR A READER TO FIND, it was taken off the headings of this file and not by impression, and the instrument is named so that a reader may run it: the count is **eleven** blocks headed *DECISIONS OF RECORD* in this file, one written by the Volume 06 outline phase, one by the Volume 07 outline phase, one by the Volume 07 close phase, one by the Volume 08 outline phase, one by the Volume 08 close phase, one by the Volume 09 outline phase, one by the Volume 09 close phase, one by the Volume 10 outline phase, one by the Volume 10 close phase, one by the Volume 11 outline phase, and the eleventh is the Volume 12 outline phase, which is at the foot of this file below this one. **AND THE INSTRUMENT IS NAMED BECAUSE IT DID NOT AGREE WITH THE COUNT TWICE, AND THE DISAGREEMENT IS PUBLISHED AND NOT RESOLVED BY DELETING IT: a count run over second-level headings alone returns ten against eleven blocks, and it returns ten because the Volume 06 outline phase's block is headed at the third level, where it was written before the ten below it, and that heading is at the second level now with its first edition preserved in its own words, so that the count reproduces at eleven by the instrument a reader reaches for and did not before this pass. A MEASUREMENT THAT SAID TEN IS WRONG ABOUT THE BLOCKS AND RIGHT ABOUT THE INSTRUMENT, and this file now satisfies both, and the block it counted as missing is at the top of this file and has been since the Volume 06 outline phase wrote it.** **AND THE CLAUSE THIS PASS SUPERSEDES IS PRESERVED IN ITS OWN WORDS AND IS NOT DELETED: *THE CORRECTION IS PUBLISHED HERE FOR THE SECOND TIME RATHER THAN LEFT FOR A READER TO FIND: the count is eleven blocks headed DECISIONS OF RECORD in this file, taken by name and not by impression: one for the Volume 06 outline, one for the Volume 07 outline, one for the Volume 07 close, two for Volume 08 (the close and then the outline), two for Volume 09 (the close and then the outline), two for Volume 10 (the close and then the outline), one for the Volume 11 outline, and the eleventh is the Volume 12 outline, which is at the foot of this file below this one.*** **AND THE FIRST EDITION OF THAT CLAUSE IS PRESERVED IN ITS OWN WORDS AND IS NOT DELETED: *the count is ten blocks headed DECISIONS OF RECORD in this file, taken by name and not by impression: one for the Volume 06 outline, one for the Volume 07 outline, one for the Volume 07 close, two for Volume 08 (the close and then the outline), two for Volume 09 (the close and then the outline), two for Volume 10 (the close and then the outline), and the tenth is the Volume 11 outline, which is at the foot of this file below this one.*** A count of the kind of thing this repository exists to catch has now been caught four times in the sentence that tells a careful reader how to find their way around the argument, and the fourth of the four was not the figure this sentence is about at all but the instrument the figure was taken with, and a silent correction would be the fifth version of the same fault. **AND THE TWO EDITIONS OF THAT SENTENCE THAT THIS PASS SUPERSEDES ARE PRESERVED IN THEIR OWN WORDS AND ARE NOT DELETED, one after the other and in the order they were written: *A count of the kind of thing this repository exists to catch has now been caught three times in the sentence that tells a careful reader how to find their way around the argument, and it is corrected here rather than left, and a silent correction is the third version of the same fault.* And then, whole and unaltered: *A count of the kind of thing this repository exists to catch has now been caught twice in the sentence that tells a careful reader how to find their way around the argument, and it is corrected here rather than left, and a silent correction is the third version of the same fault.*** THE FIRST EDITION OF THE INSTRUCTION IS PRESERVED IN ITS OWN WORDS: *a reader who wants the series and not the argument should skip all nine blocks, and the count is nine blocks headed DECISIONS OF RECORD in this file.* AND THIS PARAGRAPH OPENED WITH A MARK THAT NOTHING CLOSED, and it is closed here, and the fault is published rather than tidied: this is the third paragraph in this repository with an unbalanced bold mark at the head of a state or outline file that a phase found and routed, and the other two are at `state/continuity.md` at its forty-eighth line and at `state/open-threads.md` at its third, and all three were one marker each, and all three were open at the head of a paragraph and never closed at the foot of one, and the ten paragraphs above this one that open a block the same way all close, and this one did not.**

**AND THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK IS NOT, repeated from the close record and repeated here because a decision block is a page a writer reads and this file is the second such page: the name of the volume before the last, and the words the man of about thirty-four says in Chapter 0428; that volume's one rule, the panel of day 438; the answer spoken in Chapter 0357; the refusal spoken in Chapter 0393, whose six words are also reproduced at `bible/power-system.md:1684` and at `outline/volume-08.md:208`; the day-154 sentence; the count of chapters carrying the day-154 beat, which the Volume 09 plan withdrew; and the words the man of about twenty-seven speaks in Chapter 0486, which are this volume's own name and which are on the page of Chapter 0486 and in no file. None of them is printed here and none of them is paraphrased here and no sentence here certifies that any of them is in no file, because `state/volume-10-close.md` §7.8 publishes the measurement that looked for all seven, with the file paths and the counts, and **two of the rows of that measurement are published as disagreements rather than resolved, and a block that inherited a file count it could not produce would be the failure this repository exists to catch.** The hold is on this phase's own files and not on where a string stands: a plan is not a page, a permanent witness section is not a page, and a batch record is not a page either. And the page a writer of Volume 11 will read first is `state/volume-10-close.md`, and the whole of what this hold buys is lost in one sentence written there.**

**DECISION ONE — THE VOICE. CONFIRMED, NINE TIMES NOW, AND IT CARRIES THE THREE INSTRUMENT RULES IT INHERITED AND THE THREE THIS VOLUME FOUND, ALL SIX OF THEM ABOUT INSTRUMENTS AND NOT ABOUT PROSE.**

**Volume 06's voice is this book's voice and it is confirmed for the rest of the run. Fifty-day volumes, one chapter to one day, about a page and a yard and a wage in salt. Nobody gets stronger. The antagonist is a method and not a form, and the third volume in a row has proved a different half of it in a room with a date on the door. The only instrument this book has that a return cannot be filled in from is a blank. There is no narrator frame, and the narrator may not say *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch* or *the reader*, and on Volume 10's forty-nine chapter files those six are at zero in every body. The range of fifty-day volumes is from here and is not a rule about the past: Volume 04 is the recorded exception and is not reopened, and a volume after that which wants a double day has to argue for it in its own outline against a rule that is now the default.**

**The three rules that came down to this close are carried whole and are not restated here at less than their length; they are in the Volume 10 block above. The three this volume found are these, and the figures are published because a finding without its figures is an impression and the instrument is printed beside every one of them.**

1. **A LIST OF DECLARED MOTIFS IS NOT AN INSTRUMENT, AND A LIST WRITTEN AFTER TWENTY CHAPTERS ARE ON DISK DESCRIBES THE LAST TEN AND NOT THE VOLUME.** `outline/volume-10.md` §20.3 declared six motifs and told every batch that its word-run check was expected to return them, that each run was to be named as one of them or as a duplication, and that a run that was not one of the six was a duplication and was to be repaired before the record was written. **The check, run at the sixteen-word threshold on all forty-nine files against a pool of 499, returns SEVENTY-FOUR maximal runs. FOUR of them are one of the six declared motifs. SEVENTY are required declarations of that same plan's own object inventory at §20.6 and of its own prose instructions at §17** — eleven of them the second slate and the register on its shelf, which §20.6's own row requires to be untouched on all fifty days; ten of them the state of the store's board, which is §20.6's first row; eight of them the date-on-the-door frame that every batch record in the volume names as the volume's declared frame. **A batch that had run §20.3 as written would have classified seventy required declarations as duplications and repaired them, and the repairs would have deleted the volume's declared facts. What is decided and binds: a declared-motif list is a thing a phase writes for itself and cannot inherit, and the cheap instrument that works is to publish each surviving run WITH THE FILE IT STANDS IN and let a reader classify it, which is what the close record's §7.3 does for all seventy-four. THE FORTY-THRESHOLD FINDING IS THE CONTROL AND IT IS PUBLISHED BESIDE IT: the same check at forty words returns exactly ONE maximal run in the whole volume, forty-six words, standing in Chapter 0468 and Chapter 0471, and it is the required full form of day 298 and it is a declared declaration and it stays.**
2. **THE PROTAGONIST IS IN FIFTEEN CHAPTERS OF FIFTY AND THE RULE THAT PUT HIM THERE BOUGHT A DIFFERENT KIND OF FURNITURE, AND THE MEASUREMENT OF HOW MUCH IS FIVE.** **He is named in fifteen of the fifty chapters — 0453, 0454, 0458, 0461, 0464, 0466, 0471, 0476, 0479, 0482, 0485, 0488, 0491, 0494 and 0496 — and in no chapter in any form in the other thirty-five, and the fifteen are the plan's own list and they reproduce at fifteen of fifteen on all forty-nine files with no sixteenth added and no chapter moved. He is in no chapter in which he reads a page and he has no quoted word at all. The rule that produced seven and then nine was struck and replaced by a rule about his hands: NO HANDS, NO CHAPTER. That rule did what it said and it is not what the rule was for.** On ten of the fifteen the page says what his hands are on and what happened to somebody else because of them and says nothing about what he wanted. **On five of the fifteen a page says what he wanted** — in 0461 he is given a barrow he could not read and the narrator's voice says he could see there was one number on it and could not have said what any part of it was a count of, and did not try, and what he had instead was the edge of a tally-board, which is an inside and is a refusal to know; in 0491 he wanted an order of operations and got it; in 0494 he could not read a stack for two mornings and read it when the wind came round onto the near end of it and was no further forward than the morning before; in 0496 he brought a plank because a plank is the answer to about nine feet of wet silt and because there was nothing else left in the stack. **Three of those five arrived by a review repair pass that was sent to fix a repeated construction and found a missing inside on its way, which is the finding: the inside was never a rule and was never a warning, and a rule is the only thing in this repository that has ever fixed one. WHAT IS DECIDED AND BINDS: THE RULE ABOUT HIS HANDS STAYS, AND THE NEXT RULE IS ONE CLAUSE LONGER, AND THE CLAUSE IS THAT A CARD MAY NOT PUT HIM IN A CHAPTER UNLESS THE CARD NAMES THE THING HIS HANDS ARE ON AND WHAT HE WANTED THE THING FOR, AND A CARD THAT CANNOT NAME THE SECOND MAY USE HIM ONCE IN A BATCH AND NOT TWICE. This is a decision and not a repair, no chapter of Volume 10 may be opened or moved to make room for one, and the whole of what it costs is that a series that spent two volumes putting its protagonist on a page will spend a third putting him in a pair of hands with a want in them.**
3. **A CASE FLAG IS WORTH THIRTY-ONE ON ONE HEAD IN ONE VOLUME AND A PUBLICATION THAT NAMES ONE FLAG SURVIVES IT.** Measured word-bounded on this volume's forty-nine files, the frame that carries the volume's date-on-the-door: **case-sensitive 30 in 21 files, case-insensitive 40 in 24.** The same flag on the frame beside it is worth **twenty-three occurrences and thirteen files**, at 19 in 16 against 42 in 29. **The volume's five batches published the case-insensitive figure on one phrase and the case-sensitive figure on another, in different records, and neither record said which flag it had used.** The second half of the same finding is a measurement error a two-part instrument produced: the frame this volume runs, *about nine people*, is 30 in 21 case-sensitively, the definite form *the nine people* is 4 in 4 on both, and **a bare cardinal — neither *about* nor *the* — stands 3 in 2, in Chapter 0460 once in a mouth and Chapter 0467 twice, once in a mouth and once in narration, and both of those are about a thing read out to people in a room, which is exactly what makes a reader take a bare cardinal for the frame.** A three-part measurement is not a two-part measurement with more words in it, and the two-part version of that row is what produced a published figure in the last batch record of this volume for a chapter that does not contain the phrase at all. **WHAT IS DECIDED AND BINDS: every recurring-figure row a phase publishes is published in three parts where the head admits a frame, a definite article and a bare form, and both the case flag and the frame are named on the same line as the number.**

**AND THE HOUSE FIGURES A VOLUME 11 BATCH MEASURES AGAINST, on the definition the close record re-validated at ten of ten against the last batch record's own per-file figures, which are: the share of a file's words inside bold marks, the heading line included, `---` rules counted, bold marks counted as nothing, the file split on whitespace. On Volume 10's forty-nine files: a range of 0.78 to 26.57, an unweighted mean of 16.30 and a weighted mean of 16.53 on 45,797 words in 657 paragraphs; the count of paragraphs carrying neither speaker marker is zero in all forty-nine and the count carrying a bold mark and no quotation mark is zero in all forty-nine; the exchange is 84 plain speeches and 61 answered; the longest bolded turn is ninety-three words, in Chapter 0476, which is above the working ceiling of about ninety and below the hard ceiling of ninety-five, and it is the only turn in the fifty over ninety; the longest run of speech paragraphs is four, in two files, at the ceiling; long sentences of forty words or more are 466 on both readings and the two readings are the same figure for a published reason; a volume mean of 32.1 words to the sentence; physical-action paragraphs of three or more sentences are 190 of a possible 193 and at least one in every one of the forty-nine; and there is exactly one panel in the fifty, on day 490. THE MEAN IS A CONSEQUENCE AND NOT A PLAN, no volume may read its own mean as a quality figure, and no review may certify or fail a volume on one. The two ends of Volume 10's range are two subjects: the low end is a chapter in which a man is handed a rag and nobody tells him anything and there is almost no marked speech in it, and the high end is a room in which a woman says one long thing out loud to about nine people who are already in the room, and eight of the forty-nine sit above Volume 09's published 22.82, all eight of them of that second kind.**

**What it costs, said plainly and not softened. The series keeps its voice and gets three more instrument rules it did not have. It loses the right to inherit a declared-motif list, to treat a recurring-figure row as a single number, and to run a rule about a protagonist's hands without saying what his hands are for. The only thing these three take away is the convenience of the person reading a file, and that convenience is what has been costing this manuscript a review finding for seven volumes.**

**DECISION TWO — THE LINE OF THE NEXT VOLUME, TO BE AUDITED BEFORE IT IS ADOPTED, ON THE CHAPTER FILES, WITH THE INSTRUMENT AND THE FILE COUNT PUBLISHED, AND THE POOL OF ORDINARY WORDS AT ZERO MUST BE RE-MEASURED BECAUSE THE CORPUS GREW WHILE THE LIST DID NOT.**

**The standing test is at `outline/volume-07.md` §19: a line is made of words the manuscript has never used AND of things the manuscript has never seen, and a line that is only the first collides with a page that already means something else. The Volume 10 outline phase published four readings of the word-boundary sentence and adopted the literal one — letters only, hyphen and apostrophe both delimit — because that reading reproduces the thirty-seven-row table in the Volume 09 close's fourth decision at thirty-seven of thirty-seven and the literal reading is what the published definition says. It also published the finding that the two tables in this repository were taken with two instruments under one definition, and that the tell is nameable: the rows the two readings separate are the rows that take a hyphen or an apostrophe. THIS CLOSE REPRODUCES BOTH TABLES ON BOTH READINGS AND THE FINDING STANDS, and the next outline inherits the reading and not the argument.**

**THE INSTRUMENT AND THE FILE COUNT, PUBLISHED BESIDE IT: every figure is a word-bounded count, the token bounded by whitespace, punctuation or a line end, case-sensitive unless the figure says otherwise, over whole files and not line by line, taken over ALL FOUR HUNDRED AND NINETY-NINE CHAPTER FILES. 499 is the number and it is 450 plus the forty-nine of Volume 10 and a writer who counts 450 has left the last volume out. The descriptor instrument validates itself first, and this close ran it against Volume 09's own published map and it returns all fifteen of that map at all fifteen occurrence figures and all fifteen file figures, cell for cell.**

**AND THE TWO FINDINGS THAT BIND THE NEXT OUTLINE, both of them about the same disease and both of them measured on 499 rather than inherited:**

1. **A PUBLISHED ZERO THAT IS NOT ZERO IS THE SAME FAILURE AS A WRONG COUNT, AND THIS REPOSITORY HAS NOW PROVED IT TWICE IN OPPOSITE DIRECTIONS INSIDE ONE VOLUME.** The list of fifty-six ordinary words the Volume 09 close published at zero across four hundred and fifty chapter files, for the express purpose of letting the next outline start there: **re-measured on 499, fifty-five reproduce at exactly zero and one does not, and the one is *sledge*, at seven occurrences in two files of Volume 04, and it reproduces at seven in the same two files.** The second one is new and it is this volume's own: **the word the volume turned on is no longer at zero. `outline/volume-10.md` §19.3 published the volume's zero column as twenty ordinary words and said that the word for a unit and the word for reckoning are the two the volume turns on and that both are at zero across all four hundred and fifty chapter files, and the volume built its whole claim to newness on that. Re-measured on 499, that word stands at TWO OCCURRENCES IN ONE FILE, and the file is `chapters/volume-10/chapter-0463.md`, and both are in one marked turn, in a mouth, naming the difference between a count of places and a count of people, on the day the plan itself set for the discovery that the reason is a word.** **This is not a fault and the plan's own §17.17 permits exactly that use, in a mouth naming the difference. What is now false is the published zero, the plan is the file that prints it, and a close may not edit a plan it is recording. THE RULE THE NEXT OUTLINE INHERITS: PUBLISH THE READING AND NOT ONLY THE COUNT; A MEASUREMENT TAKEN ON A WINDOW IS A FACT ABOUT THE WINDOW; A PUBLISHED ZERO IS A CLAIM ABOUT A CORPUS AND NOT A FACT ABOUT A CHAPTER, AND THE CORPUS GROWS WHILE THE LIST DOES NOT; AND EVERY LIST OF ZEROS HANDED DOWN MUST BE RE-RUN ON THE CURRENT CORPUS BEFORE A SINGLE WORD IS BUILT ON IT.**
2. **AND THE HONEST HALF OF VOLUME 10'S OWN LINE, WHICH THE PLAN PUBLISHED AND WHICH THE NEXT OUTLINE SHOULD INHERIT AS A HABIT RATHER THAN AS A FIGURE.** Every load-bearing noun of that line was in the high column of the manuscript's own vocabulary and not one of them was at zero, and the newness was one ordinary word wide. That is the same result that made Volume 08's line and Volume 09's line work, and **Volume 10 found out what one word wide costs: the word is now on a page, in a mouth, and the zero that was the plan's whole claim to newness is false. A LINE THAT TURNS ON ONE ORDINARY WORD THIS MANUSCRIPT HAS NEVER SAID IS A LINE WHOSE NEWNESS IS ONE WORD, AND THE NEXT OUTLINE SHOULD SAY OUT LOUD WHICH HALF IT IS STANDING ON INSTEAD OF DISCOVERING WHICH HALF IT WAS STANDING ON AFTERWARDS.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series gains the cheapest inheritance it has had in three volumes and a standing instruction to re-run every list it is handed, and it loses the convenience of quoting a published zero without its corpus, and of quoting a recurring figure without its flag. The pool of ordinary words at zero has to be re-measured on 499 by the next outline and not inherited from this block, and this block publishes the figure and the reading so that the re-measurement is a check and not an argument.**

**DECISION THREE — THE CALENDAR OF THE NEXT VOLUME, AND IT IS THE ONE THAT CANNOT BE SKIPPED, AND THE BARE MONTH STILL HAS NO STATED LENGTH.**

**`bible/power-system.md` §58 is the calendar for days 501 to 550, a new numbered section in the same numbered form as §57, and it amends nothing in §32, §33, §33A, §33B or §51, and it moves no day in §57, and the Volume 10 outline phase's own ruling that §57 is unamendable by any batch and by any outline phase is carried here unchanged. The form is *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day less 315, which is the sixth form and is the one registered at §33A item 2 and named on a page in Chapter 0316 by Sera Vail, a notary of fifty, twenty-six years a notary, and in no other chapter and in no other file. Day 501 is the hundred and eighty-sixth day of that month and day 550 is the two hundred and thirty-fifth, one chapter to one day, no double day, and no chapter may write another chapter's day, may not write a day before the hundred and eighty-sixth or after the two hundred and thirty-fifth, and may not use an ordinal for a month.**

**AND THE DECISION INSIDE THE DECISION, and it is the same one six close phases in a row have now taken and for the same reason: THE BARE MONTH DOES NOT END INSIDE VOLUME 11, AND §58 GIVES IT NO LENGTH AND SAYS SO IN A SENTENCE. The month was registered with no stated length by a notary of fifty who named it on a page, said why, and the reason is hers, and the protection that bought is that no later volume can inherit an end by accident. Volume 09 wrote the eighty-sixth to the hundred and thirty-fifth of it and Volume 10 wrote the hundred and thirty-sixth to the hundred and eighty-fifth, and NEITHER ENDED IT AND NEITHER ATTACHED A MEANING TO ANY ROUND ORDINAL, and a hundred and thirty-six to a hundred and eighty-five is the longest stretch any volume in this series has spent inside one month, and fifty chapters fitted inside it without approaching an end, and that is the strongest evidence this repository has that the device does not run out. No chapter may say the month is nearly over, may not say what it is a month of, may not convert an ordinal into a span, may not print *a hundred and eighty-five days* or *six months* or *since the month began* as a figure, and may not say what the month does at its end, how many days it has, or whether it is long or short. THE WORDS *LENGTH*, *LONG* AND *SHORT* APPLIED TO THE MONTH STAY AT ZERO.**

**And the rule for whoever comes after, unchanged in substance and restated because it is the point of deciding it here: a seventh form exists the moment the end of the Bare Month is on a page, and it comes onto a page the same way the sixth one did — a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, who says the cost out loud first and is refused by somebody before she does it, and who then declines to say how long the new month is and says why. The Short Month and the Bare Month were both made that way, in Chapter 0256 and in Chapter 0316, by the same notary of fifty, and a calendar is not a decision an outline takes and a batch guesses. IT IS A DECISION A PERSON MAKES IN A ROOM, AND EVERY FORM IN THIS BOOK IS ONE, INCLUDING THE ONES IN THE BIBLE. A volume that needs a seventh form and has not got the end of the Bare Month on a page writes inside the Bare Month and IT MAY NOT DECLARE THE MONTH OVER. NO OUTLINE PHASE MAY MAKE THE SEVENTH FORM, AND NO CLOSE PHASE EITHER, AND THIS BLOCK DOES NOT MAKE IT AND DOES NOT GUESS AT IT.**

**What it costs. The series gets a calendar it can plan fifty days against and it pays for it with a seventh form that still does not exist: Volume 11 is a volume that begins on an unknown number of days into a month of no known length and ends on another one, and the protection that buys is that no volume can inherit an end by accident. A hundred and eighty-five days of a month of no stated length is the longest stretch this series has spent inside one month and the next one will be longer, and that is the cost and it is the reason the device has not run out in three volumes.**

**DECISION FOUR — THE CAST, AND THE INSTRUMENT, AND THE POOL, AND THE ONE THING THIS VOLUME FOUND ABOUT THE POOL THAT THE NEXT OUTLINE MUST ACT ON.**

**No name and no place arrives by narration in this book. It is checked for a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person. The instrument is the oldest one in the book and it is unchanged: a document arrives and is read out, and the person who reads it out says out loud what it is for before anybody asks, and the four days of notice is what brings people to a room, and a notice on any other day is a three-day instrument and a three-day instrument does not fail, it arrives warm. And there is no eighth notice in this flood, and Volume 10 sent none either, and the page that says so is `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0389.md:35`, and the word *notice* and the word *notices* stand at two and three across this volume's forty-nine files in four of them, and not one of the fifty writes an eighth.**

**THE POOL, RE-MEASURED ON ALL FOUR HUNDRED AND NINETY-NINE CHAPTER FILES, AND IT IS ZERO, AND THE CLEAN POOL FOR A NEW PERSON IS ZERO.** The instrument validates itself against Volume 09's published map at fifteen of fifteen on every cell. **Eighty-five distinct descriptors stand across all 499 chapter files. Volume 10's forty-nine files carry FIFTEEN distinct descriptors, which is the same count as Volume 09's fifty and the smallest map in this series. Applying the Volume 10 plan's own rule — a descriptor is available to a new person only if it stands at three occurrences or fewer across every chapter file, every one of those occurrences is a passing mention and not a scene, and it is at zero in each of the last three volumes' fifty — RETURNS ZERO.** Volume 10 added no new person, took no descriptor for anybody and invented nothing.

**AND THE ONE PERSON THIS VOLUME PUT ON A PAGE PROPERLY IS A PERSON WHO WAS ALREADY ON ONE AND WHO HAS NO AGE AND NO DESCRIPTOR ON ANY PAGE IN THIS MANUSCRIPT, and she was put there by being asked, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, and she is on a page three times in the fifty and is given nothing — no name, no number, no descriptor, and no question about why she stopped. THAT IS THE ONLY MOVE LEFT INSIDE THIS MANUSCRIPT'S OWN RULES AND IT IS THE ONE THE NEXT OUTLINE HAS TO LEARN FROM RATHER THAN REDISCOVER.**

**THE PROBLEM TO NAME AND NOT SOLVE, carried forward in the plan's own words because it is still true and it is still unsolved: a new person in this book will have to arrive without a number, the way the woman who walked a market arrived, and A WRITER WHO CANNOT SEE HOW TO DO THAT HAS TO SAY SO IN ITS OWN OUTLINE RATHER THAN QUIETLY ATTACH A DESCRIPTOR TO SOMEBODY WHO ALREADY HAS ONE. The pool is not replenished from inside this manuscript at this rate, and the usable set has fallen from something in the fifties to thirty-seven to zero in three volumes, and the thirty-seven were never thirty-seven and the fifties were never fifties, and the real number has been zero for some time and no instrument in this repository was looking in the right place.**

**AND THE TWO DESCRIPTOR DEBTS CARRY, and this close re-measured both on 499 and neither is paid. FIRST: the figure *about two hundred rooms* stands at TWENTY OCCURRENCES IN SIXTEEN FILES across the whole manuscript — ten in six files of Volume 08 exactly as the Volume 09 close published them, and TEN IN TEN DISTINCT FILES OF VOLUME 07 THAT THE PUBLISHED ROW DID NOT NAME — and its attribution is unpaid. THE RULE IS SETTLED AND BINDS WHATEVER COMES NEXT: the figure is not available to a new person, no chapter after this one may give it to anybody, and it stands at ZERO across this volume's forty-nine files. SECOND: no chapter of Volume 10 printed a room count for anybody, in narration or in a mouth, on a list of cardinals or on a list that includes *dozen*; the word *rooms* stands twice across the forty-nine and both of the two are general references and neither is a count, and *dozen* is at zero, and the one room count printed in Volume 09 at `chapters/volume-09/chapter-0416.md:11` is still unpaid and is routed to a Volume 09 review repair pass and is not this phase's.**

**AND WHERE ADRIAN VALE IS, WHICH IS DECISION ONE AND IS NOT REPEATED HERE: fifteen chapters of fifty, no chapter in which he reads a page, no quoted word of his in any of them, aged nowhere, Stage 2 on all fifty days, and the rule about his hands now carries the second clause that names what he wanted the thing for.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses a measured pool it can draw from, and the pool turns out never to have been one, and it gains a cast decision it can act on: a new person arrives without a number or the outline says in its own words that it cannot write one. A volume about a count is a volume about people a count is about who are not in the room, and the instrument for putting a person in this book is a question asked in daylight with a date on the door, and that is what this volume spent fifty days on and it spent it correctly.**

**AND THE FIVE DEBTS THIS CLOSE NAMES AND PAYS NONE, because a close phase that quietly takes a routed item to save itself a line is how nineteen quotation marks became a four-volume debt, and this is the fifth volume that has said that sentence: `chapters/volume-10/chapter-0475.md` DOES NOT EXIST and is owed to the next OUTLINE phase or to a Volume 11 close and to no writer and no repair pass; VOLUME 10'S FOUR CARD FILES are owed to the next OUTLINE phase, named here one by one because the first edition of this sentence said only *the four card files at `outline/batches/`* and a reader could take that repo-wide, and the repo-wide figure is much larger and is published so that the two readings cannot be confused: of the fifty batches in this manuscript that have chapters on disk, forty-two have no card file at all, and of Volume 10's own five, `volume-10-batch-0001.md` exists and was written before the outline, `volume-10-batch-0002.md` exists and is registered as batch-authored and pending ratification and not ratified, and `-0003.md`, `-0004.md` and `-0005.md` do not exist, and the four Volume 10 owes are those three absences and that one unratified file, and not forty-two; the published zero for the word the volume turned on is owed to the next OUTLINE phase; the declared-motif list that is not an instrument is owed to the next OUTLINE phase; AND THE FIFTH IS NEW TO THIS PARAGRAPH AND IS A WITHDRAWN FIGURE STILL STANDING IN A LIVE PLAN FILE: `outline/volume-10.md:419` still prints *three hundred and forty-nine plus fifty less one ... which is three hundred and ninety-eight*, which is the same off-by-one the first edition of this close's own prompt published twice, and the line above it in that plan gives 348 at day 450, and the correct figure is three hundred and ninety-seven on both routes, and A PLAN FILE IS A FILE AND A CLOSE MAY NOT EDIT A PLAN IT IS RECORDING, so the repair is owed to the next OUTLINE phase, which may amend that one cell or record the correction in its own outline, and never silently. NONE OF THE FIVE IS OWED BY A WRITER AND NONE IS OWED BY A REPAIR PASS.**

**AND THE TWO CONTROLLER FAULTS, named and not worked around, because they are not this phase's to touch: `state/phase-ledger.json` has never moved past `phase-000-bootstrap` and `status: planned` after something near a hundred completed phases, and `scripts/novel_runner.sh` derives its phase id with `basename`, so every phase whose directory is called the same thing shares one log file and one branch name — AND THE BLAST RADIUS OF THE SECOND ONE, WHICH A REVIEW OF THIS BLOCK FOUND UNDERSTATED, BECAUSE THIS BLOCK ORIGINALLY NAMED ONLY `batch-0005` AND THE COLLISION IS NOT ABOUT `batch-0005`: of the sixty-six phase directories in this repository that hold a prompt, `basename` returns ten identical ids for `batch-0001` and ten for `batch-0003`, nine each for `batch-0002` and `batch-0004`, eight for `batch-0005`, SEVEN FOR `close` AND SEVEN FOR `outline`, and one each for the other five, so sixty-one of the sixty-six directories share one of seven ids with at least one other directory, and the remaining five are the only phases in this repository whose log and branch are their own. `logs/close.log` is a single file of about nine hundred and thirty-five kilobytes shared by all seven close phases, and `novel-wip/close` is one branch for all of them, and this volume's own close is one of the seven, and this volume's own last batch record contains the log line that the day-154 span sits in, which is the same log. Both files are in the list this agent may not edit, neither was opened, and both are named for the owner of the workflow. A phase that finds a fault in a controller and quietly works around it has made the controller worse. THEY ARE REFERRED AND NOT ROUTED, AND A REPAIR IS NOT OWED BY THE NEXT PHASE EITHER.**

**AND THE NEXT PHASE, BY PATH: `workspace/volume-11/outline/PROMPT.md`, and it is a VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE and not a batch. It writes `outline/volume-11.md`, Volume 11's first batch's cards at `outline/batches/volume-11-batch-0001.md`, its own block in this file, and no prose. It is the only kind of phase after a close that is allowed to decide, and this close has left it the nine decisions of record this volume inherited, the four this close added, the FIVE debts it must act on or route, of which the fifth is new and was routed by the review repair pass at `state/volume-10-close.md` §8 item 5, the six open questions at `state/volume-10-close.md` §9, and a card-file arrangement it inherits and does not reopen. It amends nothing in `bible/power-system.md` §51 or §57, it may not make a seventh form, it may not move a day, it may not print any of the seven held things this block has named and not printed, and it may not edit `outline/volume-10.md` to repair the two rows this close measured, save the one cell this block names at the fifth debt, which it may amend or record and may never do silently.**


## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 11 — ADDED AND AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 11 OUTLINE PHASE, in its own words

**The Volume 10 close left four decisions open and took four of its own, and this block is the sixth set this volume inherited and the sixth it added, and the whole of it is in `outline/volume-11.md`, which is the plan and not this block. THIS BLOCK IS THE SUMMARY AND THE SUMMARY IS NOT THE PLAN, and a writer of Volume 11 reads `outline/volume-11.md` and not this file, and this file is here so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost.**

**AND THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK IS NOT, repeated from the close record and from the block above because a decision block is a page a writer reads and this file is the second such page: the name of the volume before the last, and the words the man of about thirty-four says in Chapter 0428; that volume's one rule, the panel of day 438; the answer spoken in Chapter 0357; the refusal spoken in Chapter 0393, whose six words are also reproduced at `bible/power-system.md:1684` and at `outline/volume-08.md:208`; the day-154 sentence; the count of chapters carrying the day-154 beat, which the Volume 09 plan withdrew; and the words the man of about twenty-seven speaks in Chapter 0486, which are the volume before this one's own name. None of them is printed here and none of them is paraphrased here and no sentence here certifies that any of them is in no file, because `state/volume-10-close.md` §7.8 publishes the measurement that looked for all seven, with the file paths and the counts, and two of its rows are published as disagreements rather than resolved. The hold is on each phase's own files and not on where a string stands: a plan is not a page, a permanent witness section is not a page, and a batch record is not a page either. AND THE MEASUREMENT OF THE HOLD IS AT `state/volume-10-close.md` §7.8, AND IT WAS NOT RE-RUN BY THIS PHASE, BECAUSE RE-RUNNING IT REQUIRES PRINTING THE SPANS AND THIS BLOCK MAY NOT PRINT ONE, AND A PHASE THAT COULD NOT MEASURE THE HOLD AND SAID SO IS BETTER THAN A PHASE THAT MEASURED IT AND LEAKED IT.**

**DECISION ONE, INHERITED AND CARRIED — THE VOICE, CONFIRMED FOR THE TENTH TIME, and it now carries seven instrument rules and not three.** Fifty-day volumes, one chapter to one day, about a page and a yard and a wage in salt. Nobody gets stronger. The antagonist is a method and not a form. The only instrument this book has that a return cannot be filled in from is a blank. No narrator frame, and the narrator may not say *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch* or *the reader*. The house figures a Volume 11 batch measures against are published in the block above and are not reprinted, and **the mean is a consequence and not a plan and no volume may read its own mean as a quality figure and no review may certify or fail a volume on one.**

**AND THE INSTRUMENT RULE THIS PHASE ADDED, which is the eighth in the running and the first that is about a plan rather than about a page: A DECISION NOT TAKEN IS A DECISION, AND A PLAN THAT CANNOT SAY WHAT IT DECIDED NOT TO DO CANNOT BE CHECKED BY THE PHASE THAT COMES AFTER IT. Every decision this phase declined to take is named in `outline/volume-11.md` §21 with the reason, and the two it took by declining bind as hard as the four it took by choosing, and the sentence exists because `outline/volume-10.md` §6.4 said a version of it about one decision and a reader in a year cannot tell a decision not taken from an oversight.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series gets a plan that a reviewer can check against its own abstentions, and it pays for it with a file that is longer than the one before it and that a batch will not read to the end. The convenience of a plan that only lists what it decided is the convenience that has cost this repository a review finding for seven volumes.**

**DECISION TWO, TAKEN BY THIS PHASE — THE LINE, audited on four hundred and ninety-nine chapter files before it was adopted, with the instrument and the file count published, and the count is 499 and not 450 and it was re-derived in this run.**

**A figure of this city is counted on foot by a person who is on no roll and has no name and cannot be shown to anybody, it is put in the one place under a heading on a form, and the form leaves this city. The figure that came four hundred miles off is still on a wall anybody can read. The two do not match and are not allowed to. Four counts of the same kind of thing have been made on four mornings and all four have been entered one under another in the second column of this city's only public book, and the column reads down, and the wall reads across, and no instrument in four hundred miles can say which of the two a person should be using.**

**AND THE HALF THE LINE IS STANDING ON, SAID OUT LOUD, BEFORE A CHAPTER EXISTS, which is the sentence the volume before this one needed and did not say: THE LINE IS STANDING ON THE THINGS AND NOT ON THE WORDS. Every load-bearing noun of it is in the high column of this manuscript's own vocabulary, and one of them is in the highest, and a line built on nouns in the high column is a line about the oldest thing in this book, and this plan is not embarrassed about that and does not dress it up as novelty. The word column is one word wide, it is at zero on all four hundred and ninety-nine chapter files on both case readings, it is ordinary, it is in the local register, and IT IS GOING TO BE SPENT ON A PAGE IN A MOUTH IN A ROOM IN DAYLIGHT WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, AND THIS BLOCK SAYS SO BEFORE A CHAPTER EXISTS INSTEAD OF LETTING A CLOSE FIND IT AFTERWARDS, WHICH IS WHAT THE VOLUME BEFORE THIS ONE COST. A zero published about a corpus is a claim about a corpus and a zero a volume spends deliberately is the only kind worth publishing.**

**AND THE FOUR THINGS THE MANUSCRIPT HAS NEVER HAD ON A PAGE, named so that a writer can write one and a reviewer can fail it: a form with one place under a heading, whose one place is to be filled in, that is then sent OUT of this city, when every document this flood has produced has travelled inward from an office nine hundred miles off; one column of one book with five figures of the same kind of thing standing in it, one under another, in the order they were made, and the top one not struck, so that a book reads down where a wall reads across and this manuscript has never put those two surfaces in one room; a figure of this city counted on foot and entered four times where the four do not agree and are not permitted to agree, which no page in this flood has entered into a book; and a form with no place under the heading for the person who counted the figure, going out of a city the person is standing in, correct about the right people, which is one step on from a form that went out correct about the wrong people two volumes ago and the step is the whole of a volume.**

**AND WHAT IT WAS MEASURED AGAINST, and every figure carries its reading and its corpus. The inherited list of fifty-six ordinary words at zero, re-run on 499, word-bounded, case-sensitive, letters only: fifty-five reproduce at exactly zero and one does not, and the one is *sledge* at seven in two files of Volume 04, which reproduces the plan's own finding and is repaired by nobody. The other direction, which nobody runs: the volume before this one's own zero column came back with one word non-zero at two occurrences in one file of that volume, in a mouth, naming the difference, on the day the plan itself set, and the use is permitted and the published zero was false, and this phase has amended that one cell of that plan in place with its first edition preserved beneath the correction. And a third direction nobody runs and this phase ran: a figure that comes back non-zero where a fault was expected, which is the descriptor count, and it is published rather than passed.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series gains a line it can be checked against on two surfaces instead of one, and it loses the convenience of inheriting a list of zeros, and it takes on the obligation of a word it is going to spend and the cost of saying so in advance. It also takes on a debt it did not ask for: the descriptor count at §19.6 of the plan does not reproduce against the close's published figure, and both are published.**

**DECISION THREE, INHERITED AND CONFIRMED — THE CALENDAR. `bible/power-system.md` §58 is the calendar for days 501 to 550 and it is unamended, and this phase re-ran its fifty rows out of day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else and checked them on chapter-equals-day, on weekday, and on the Bare-Month ordinal being day less 315, and got FIFTY ROWS AT ZERO MISMATCHES, and neither §57's table nor §58's table was consulted until after the run was built. Day 501 is a Friday and the hundred and eighty-sixth day of the Bare Month; day 550 is a Friday and the two hundred and thirty-fifth; one chapter to one day, no double day. The form is *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day less 315, which is the sixth form and was named on a page in Chapter 0316 by a notary of fifty who declined to say how long that month is and said why. THE BARE MONTH DOES NOT END INSIDE VOLUME 11 AND §58 GIVES IT NO LENGTH. NO OUTLINE PHASE AND NO CLOSE PHASE MAY MAKE THE SEVENTH FORM, THIS PHASE DID NOT MAKE IT, DID NOT GUESS AT IT, AND GAVE THE BARE MONTH NOTHING.**

**AND THE ONE INPUT THIS PHASE CLOSED, because a plan that decides a panel and does not fix its day leaves a formula with an input still open and a formula with an input still open is not a figure: THIS VOLUME'S ONE PANEL IS ON DAY 540, A TUESDAY, THE TWO HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FIFTH DAY OF THAT MONTH, AND IT IS THE ONLY DAY INSIDE 501 TO 550 ON WHICH THE WALL SAYS ANYTHING. The count of this city's silences is therefore now a figure and not a formula, and both routes are published in the plan and agree, and the figure is a state-layer figure, is printed in no chapter, and is NOT CARRIED INTO THE BATCH PROMPT THIS PHASE CREATES.**

**AND THE ARITHMETIC THAT IS A TRAP AND THAT NO OTHER FILE IN THIS REPOSITORY NAMES: day 540 is also the two hundred and twenty-fifth day of the Bare Month, and the two hundred and twenty-fifth day of this flood is the day the store's board was last left unwritten, and the two are different numbers that look the same. No chapter of Volume 11 may print both, join them, or give either of them a meaning.**

**What it costs. The series gets a calendar it can plan fifty days against, a count of silences that is a figure, and a panel day that four hundred mornings of a washed board are not sitting next to by accident. It pays with a seventh form that still does not exist, and with the knowledge that a hundred and eighty-six to a two hundred and thirty-fifth day of a month of no stated length is the longest stretch this series has spent inside one month and that the next one will be longer.**

**DECISION FOUR, INHERITED AND CARRIED, AND ONE NEW CAST DECISION — THE POOL IS ZERO BY A READING AND NOT BY A SWEEP, AND THE TWO ARE PUBLISHED SEPARATELY FOR THE FIRST TIME.** No name and no place arrives by narration in this book. It is checked for a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person. The instrument is the oldest one in the book and is unchanged: a document arrives and is read out, and the person who reads it out says out loud what it is for before anybody asks, and there is no eighth notice in this flood and Volume 11 sends none.

**The standing rule for a new person's descriptor has three halves and only two of them are arithmetic: at three occurrences or fewer across every chapter file; every one of those occurrences a passing mention and not a scene; and at zero in each of the last three volumes' fifty. ON ALL FOUR HUNDRED AND NINETY-NINE CHAPTER FILES THE ARITHMETIC RETURNS TWENTY-ONE DESCRIPTORS AND THE READING RETURNS ZERO, and every one of the twenty-one is attached to a person who has a page or a scene in a finished volume. THE CLEAN POOL FOR A NEW PERSON IS ZERO, AND IT IS ZERO BY THE READING, AND THE READING IS ABOUT THIRTYY-ONE LINES IN SEVEN FINISHED VOLUMES, AND NO SCRIPT IN THIS REPOSITORY CAN DO IT. A POOL THAT IS DESCRIBED BY ITS ARITHMETIC ALONE IS A POOL OF PEOPLE, and the three volumes running that have published this zero as a count have published the cheap half of the rule and called it the finding.**

**AND THE DESCRIPTOR COUNT DOES NOT REPRODUCE AGAINST THE CLOSE'S PUBLISHED FIGURE, AND BOTH ARE PUBLISHED: this phase's run returns eighty distinct descriptors on four hundred and ninety-nine files where `state/volume-10-close.md` §7.9 publishes eighty-five, and fourteen on Volume 10's forty-nine files where that record publishes fifteen, and the instrument validates at fifteen of fifteen on Volume 09's own published map, and the corpus is the same four hundred and ninety-nine files, therefore the difference is in the number resolver and not in the corpus. Neither figure is corrected and no record is edited, and a phase that published one without the other would be doing the third version of the same fault.**

**AND VOLUME 11 ADDS NO NEW PERSON, and the number is zero for the second volume running, and the reason is not a failure: a volume about a figure leaving this city needs a person who can count a thing on foot, and this manuscript has exactly one such person, and she is already on a page, and she has no age, no number and no descriptor on any page in this manuscript, and the cast decision is therefore ZERO NEW PEOPLE AND ONE RETURNING PERSON MOVED FROM THE EDGE OF A ROOM TO THE MIDDLE OF A TASK, and the cost of the move is named in the plan: she is on more pages in this volume than in the three before it put her together, and she is thanked by nobody, and she has no name on the form, and nobody in four hundred miles can be shown her, and nobody asks her why she stopped.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series loses a measured pool it thought it could draw from and gains a cast decision it can act on, and it takes on the standing risk that a volume about a count keeps needing a person this manuscript has no other way to name, and that the next phase will meet the same wall and will not have a reading to hand it.**

**DECISION FIVE, TAKEN BY THIS PHASE — THE THING THIS VOLUME SPENDS, AND WHOSE IT IS, said before the first card because a plan that cannot say what it spends is a plan that will spend a person by accident.**

**Volume 11 spends a figure's ability to stay in this city, and it spends it by sending the figure out of this city on a form. A sheet with a heading and one place under it came up the road, was read out in a room with a date on the door, the one place was filled with a figure of this city made on foot, and the form went out of this city and is not coming back and nothing in this flood has an instrument for bringing a figure back.**

**AND THE ANSWER THE CLOSE ASKED FOR AND DID NOT GET: THE COST IS PAID BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS A PUBLIC REGISTER, AND BY THE WOMAN WHO WALKED A MARKET, AND BY NEITHER MAN IN THE ROOM. The register-keeper decides the midpoint, names the cost out loud first, and is thanked by nobody. The woman who counted is thanked by nobody, is not asked anything, is on no roll, and is not on the form. THE MAN WHO WASHES THE BOARD IS IN THIS VOLUME AND IS NOT ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT.**

**AND THE DIFFERENCE FROM THE VOLUME BEFORE THIS ONE, published because the shape of this book's costs is the thing the close was really asking about: Volume 10 spent a board's silence by writing on it, and the cost was paid by the man who had kept it silent and not by the woman who decided to spend it. Volume 11's decider names the bill before she signs it, and the bill is not for the man who keeps the thing clean. THE THING THIS VOLUME SPENDS IS SPENT BY THE PERSON WHO DECIDES TO SPEND IT AND BY THE PERSON IT IS ABOUT, AND NEITHER OF THEM IS THE PERSON WHO KEEPS IT.**

**And the finding the spend buys, which is the volume: a form with no place under the heading for the person who counted it does not merely go out wrong. It goes out RIGHT. Volume 08's finding was that such a form goes out correct about the wrong people. Volume 10's was that a figure is a unit and a count and a unit is a decision somebody else made. **Volume 11's is that a figure made by a person of this city, on foot, with her own time, counted four times and entered four times, is a figure that is right, and the form has no place for her, and there is therefore nobody in four hundred miles who can be shown who made it, and nobody who can check it, and nobody who can be shown that it was made by anybody at all.** THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN WRONG AND UNSHOWABLE IS A WHOLE VOLUME, BECAUSE A WRONG FIGURE HAS AN ERROR IN IT AND AN ERROR CAN BE FOUND, AND THIS ONE HAS NO ERROR IN IT AND FOUR MORNINGS OF WEATHER IN IT, AND A READER FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF IS HOLDING THE BEST FIGURE OF THIS CITY ANYBODY HAS EVER SENT OUT OF THIS CITY AND CANNOT BE SHOWN A PERSON.

**What it costs. The series gains a volume about a form and not about a wall, a count that is reconciled four different ways and sent anyway, and a cost whose owner is named in advance instead of discovered afterwards. It pays with a figure that leaves the city, which is the first figure of this city that has ever left this city, and with a word the volume turns on that is at zero today and will not be at zero when the volume closes, and it says so now.**

**DECISION SIX, TAKEN BY THIS PHASE — THE SHAPE OF THE PROTAGONIST, AND THE CLAUSE MOVED FROM THE CARD INTO THE PLAN.**

**Adrian Vale is in ELEVEN chapters of fifty: 0501, 0504, 0509, 0512, 0517, 0521, 0529, 0535, 0543, 0546 and 0549, and the list is published in the day map's own column, which is part of the map and not a note on it, and a card may not put him in a chapter the map does not have him in. ELEVEN AND NOT FIFTEEN, and the reason is a fact about the volume: four mornings of a count on foot over eleven miles of flats, one form filled once, one figure leaving the city once, and a protagonist who is on a page on every market morning is a man who is furniture in a market, which is what the volume before this one measured at ten of fifteen.**

**THE RULE ABOUT HIS HANDS STANDS AND THE SECOND CLAUSE IS ADOPTED WHOLE: a card may not put him in a chapter unless the card names the thing his hands are on AND WHAT HE WANTED THE THING FOR, and a card that cannot name the second may use him once in a batch and not twice. AND THIS PHASE MOVES THE SECOND CLAUSE OUT OF THE CARD AND INTO THE PLAN, which is a decision about where the burden sits and not about him: the eleven wants are published in the plan, one row per chapter, so that a card never has to supply the want and can only take it or leave the chapter alone, and a review can check the quotation against one table in one line. IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS REPOSITORY THAT THE THING A RULE ASKS A CARD TO PRODUCE HAS BEEN PUT IN THE PLAN, AND IT IS PUT IN THE PLAN BECAUSE THE MEASUREMENT BEHIND IT IS FIVE OF FIFTEEN AND THE THREE OF THOSE FIVE THAT MATTERED MOST ARRIVED BY A REPAIR PASS SENT TO FIX SOMETHING ELSE.**

**Nobody in this volume gets stronger, he performs no working, no threshold is opened, the other world is not named once, nobody offers him a workway and he asks for none, and in every one of the eleven he is the cause of at least one thing that happens to somebody else, and nobody thanks him, nobody tells him he was right, and nobody in that chapter is waiting for him to be useful and he is not useful to them.**

**What it costs. The protagonist loses three chapters and gains a want on a page in eleven of them, and a reviewer gains a table. What he does not gain is a reason anybody wants him there, and the volume is not going to give him one, and that is the standing prohibition and it is not weakened by a clause about what he wanted a rag for.**

**DECISION SEVEN, TAKEN BY THIS PHASE — THE PRESSURE ROTATION, THE OBJECT INVENTORY, AND THE THREE THINGS THAT WERE WRONG WITH THEM LAST TIME.**

**The rotation is eleven physical work in weather, twelve character and relationship not about a figure, eleven political or procedural, five discovery, five decision including the midpoint and the climax and the resolution and the new question and the last image, three cost and consequence, and three recovery. Eleven plus twelve plus eleven plus five plus five plus three plus three is fifty, and the fifty chapters are fifty distinct chapters, and the table is checked cell by cell against the pressure column in the plan's own day map, because Volume 09's table listed one chapter in two rows and omitted another from every row and the counts summed to fifty over forty-nine distinct chapters and went unrepaired through two batches. Physical work is up from seven and character is down from twenty-one, and the reason is that this volume's work is on feet and on flats and not in a market. AND THE CHECK EARNED ITS KEEP IN THIS PHASE'S OWN FILE: the rotation table and the day map did not agree on their first pass — the table carried 0529 under physical work and the day map carried it as political — and running the two against each other found it, and the day map is right and the table was corrected, and that is the second volume running in this repository whose rotation table and day map have disagreed and the first in which it was found by the phase that wrote both of them rather than by a close a hundred days later.**

**THE OBJECT INVENTORY CARRIES ITS SECOND COLUMN, ITS THIRD COLUMN AS AN INTENTION, AND — FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THIS REPOSITORY — A PER-CHAPTER COLUMN, which is fifty rows indexed by chapter, because a writer holds a chapter and not an object, and the volume before this one's third column was indexed only by object and was wrong in nine of fourteen rows with eleven chapters named that never named the object and one named that did, and no batch could see that before writing and no close could see it except afterwards. A PER-VOLUME THIRD COLUMN IS A PLAN AND NOT AN INSTRUMENT, AND THE CHEAP VERSION OF THE FIX IS A PER-CHAPTER COLUMN, AND THE PLAN PUBLISHES BOTH.**

**AND THE DECLARED-MOTIF LIST IS SUPERSEDED AND NOT AMENDED, and this is the second most expensive decision in this block. `outline/volume-10.md` §20.3 declared six motifs and told every batch that a sixteen-word run that was not one of the six was a duplication and was to be repaired; on that volume's forty-nine files seventy of the seventy-four residual runs are not one of the six and are required declarations of that same plan's own object inventory and prose instructions, and A REPAIR PASS THAT HAD RUN IT AS WRITTEN WOULD HAVE DELETED SEVENTY DECLARATIONS. THIS PHASE DID NOT AMEND IT, because a plan is not re-planned by a later phase; IT SUPERSEDED IT IN ITS OWN OUTLINE, WHERE A BATCH OF VOLUME 11 WILL READ IT, WITH A RULE THAT IS NOT A LIST: a run of forty words or more is a duplication, a run of sixteen or more is a duplication unless it is a required declaration that names the row of the object inventory or the frame that requires it, a required declaration is a run that stays, and the instrument is the run published with the file it stands in.**

**What it costs. The series keeps its rotation and gains a plan column indexed the way a writer reads, and it loses the comfort of a list of motifs that could be run as a classifier, which was never an instrument and cost a volume seventy declarations.**

**AND THE EIGHT PLACES A PANEL MAY NOT TRAVEL, which is a convention and not a finding: Volume 11's one rule lives in its outline and on the page of Chapter 0540 and is reprinted in no prompt and no batch record, because a panel copied into a prompt is a panel that gets copied into a second prompt, and then the day it says something on is a day two files disagree about. The volume's own name gets the same treatment.**

**THE FIVE DEBTS, and what this phase did with each, in one line each, and the detail is at `outline/volume-11.md` §21. THE HOLE AT `chapters/volume-10/chapter-0475.md` IS NOT PAID, NOT SUMMARISED, NOT DECIDED IN AND NOT ROUTED, AND IS OWED TO A VOLUME 11 CLOSE AND TO NO WRITER AND NO REPAIR PASS, AND THIS VOLUME'S PLAN DOES NOT NEED THE SCENE BECAUSE THE FACT IS ON A WALL FOR FIFTY DAYS. THE FOUR CARD FILES OF THE VOLUME BEFORE THIS ONE ARE NOT PAID, AND THE DECISION NOT TO PAY THEM IS PUBLISHED RATHER THAN LEFT AS AN OMISSION, AND THE REASON IS THAT A CARD FILE FOR THREE FINISHED BATCHES CANNOT BE WRITTEN AS A PLAN AND A RECONSTRUCTION OF A BATCH IS A SECOND ACCOUNT OF THIRTY FINISHED CHAPTERS THAT COULD NOT BE CHECKED AGAINST THE THING IT RECONSTRUCTS, AND THE CHEAP REPAIR IS THE ARRANGEMENT AND NOT THE THREE FILES: EVERY BATCH OF VOLUME 11 WRITES ITS TEN CARDS BEFORE ITS FIRST CHAPTER, AND THIS OUTLINE WROTE VOLUME 11'S FIRST BATCH'S CARDS AT `outline/batches/volume-11-batch-0001.md` BEFORE CHAPTER 0501 EXISTED. THE PUBLISHED ZERO THAT WAS NOT ZERO IS PAID, IN ONE CELL OF `outline/volume-10.md` §19.3, WITH THE FIRST EDITION OF THAT CELL REPRODUCED BENEATH THE CORRECTION. THE DECLARED-MOTIF LIST IS SUPERSEDED AS ABOVE. THE WITHDRAWN FIGURE STILL STANDING IN A LIVE PLAN IS PAID, IN ONE CELL OF `outline/volume-10.md` §14.5, WITH THE FIRST EDITION OF THAT CELL REPRODUCED BENEATH THE CORRECTION. NOTHING ELSE IN EITHER FILE WAS TOUCHED AND NO CHAPTER WAS REPAIRED.**

**AND THE VOLUME'S OWN NAME, ARRANGED AND NOT WRITTEN, and what is decided is everything except the answer: the room over a market, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, about nine people, on day 534 at about the ninth hour; asked of the man of about thirty-nine who trades a board of nineteen rates and took a name off it in his own hand about nine years ago and has never said so; asked by the man of about thirty-four who keeps a stall, whose own board has stood face in against his own trestle since the day he took it down and whose chalk has been in his pocket since day 298, and who has said what is wrong with a page in about nine words and been thanked by nobody three volumes running; and it is a phrase, in the local language, out loud, once, in four to eight words, and it may not contain a name, a weekday, an ordinal for a month, a numeral that is a date on a page, an institution, a body, an office, a class, a version, the word on the register-keeper's second slate, *holder*, *fence*, *claim*, *boundary*, *unit*, *reckon* or the word this volume itself turns on. NO CHAPTER MAY STATE THAT IT IS THE NAME OF ANYTHING, THE NARRATOR MAY NOT SAY IT AGAIN, NO CHAPTER MAY PRINT IT A SECOND TIME, AND NO STATE FILE, NO BATCH RECORD, NO PROMPT AND NO OUTLINE MAY RECORD THE WORDS, INCLUDING THIS BLOCK AND INCLUDING THE BATCH PROMPT THIS PHASE CREATES, AND NO FILE THIS PHASE WROTE MAY PRINT, PARAPHRASE, IMPROVE ON, DESCRIBE OR CERTIFY THE ABSENCE OF THEM. THIS BLOCK SAYS WHERE THE NAME WILL BE SAID AND STOPS THERE, AND A WRITER WHO CANNOT FIND THE WORDS BY CHAPTER 0534 HAS THIRTY-FOUR DAYS, AND THE ANSWER IS THAT HE SAYS LESS THAN THE PLAN EXPECTED AND THE VOLUME IS BETTER FOR IT.**

**AND THE TWO CONTROLLER FAULTS, named and not worked around, because they are not this phase's to touch and a repair is not owed by the next phase either: `state/phase-ledger.json` has never moved past `phase-000-bootstrap` and `status: planned` after something near a hundred completed phases, and `scripts/novel_runner.sh` derives its phase id with `basename`, so every phase whose directory carries the same name shares one log file and one branch name — AND THE BLAST RADIUS, WHICH IS SIXTY-ONE OF THE SIXTY-SIX PHASE DIRECTORIES THAT HOLD A PROMPT, WITH TEN RETURNING `batch-0001`, TEN `batch-0003`, NINE EACH `batch-0002` AND `batch-0004`, EIGHT `batch-0005`, SEVEN `close` AND SEVEN `outline`, AND FIVE THAT ARE THEIR OWN, so `logs/close.log` is one file of about nine hundred and thirty-five kilobytes shared by all seven close phases and `novel-wip/close` is one branch for all of them. Both files are in the list this phase may not edit, neither was opened, nothing in either was changed, and both are named for the owner of the workflow. A phase that finds a fault in a controller and quietly works around it has made the controller worse.**

**AND THE NEXT PHASE, BY PATH: `workspace/volume-11/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, and it is a BATCH and not an outline and not a close. It writes Chapters 0501 to 0510, days 501 to 510, from a Friday to a Sunday, and it decides nothing, invents no person, and records the words the man of about twenty-seven speaks in Chapter 0486 in no file it produces. It writes its ten cards at the head of its own state record before Chapter 0501 exists, and it authors nothing under `outline/`, and it reads its own card file at `outline/batches/volume-11-batch-0001.md` before it reads anything else and stops and says so in its record if that file is missing. It carries the thirteen decisions of record, the fifteen prohibitions, the twenty-one prose instructions, the day map and the Adrian column and the pressure column, the eleven wants, the zero notices, the anchors and the twenty-one round figures, the object inventory in both columns and the per-chapter column, the eight frames and the word-run rule at both thresholds, the three-part recurring-figure table, the seven checks and the four added, the two instruments that are not scripts, and the ten forbidden closing positions. IT IS TOLD WHAT IT MAY NOT BE TOLD BY ACCIDENT, AND THE PANEL AND THE SILENCE FIGURE ARE NOT CARRIED INTO IT AT ALL.**

**AND WHAT THIS PHASE DID NOT DO, WHICH IS THE HALF OF A BLOCK THAT MATTERS MOST: no chapter was written and no chapter was repaired, and `chapters/volume-10/chapter-0475.md` was not written and was not routed. No day, no weekday, no month day, no anchor, no form, no notice, no object count that holds, no cast member and no decision of record moved. `bible/power-system.md` was not amended in §§32, 33, 33A, 33B, 51, 57 or 58 and was not opened for any purpose but the comparison of fifty rows. `outline/ending.md` was not opened: Volume 11 introduces no new final enemy, no new cosmic layer, no new antagonist and no new world, and the antagonist ladder and the relationship milestones are untouched. No card file was written for any batch but this outline's own first one. No new person was invented, no descriptor was taken, and no person arrived by narration. No file this phase wrote prints, paraphrases, improves on, describes or certifies the absence of any of the seven held things named at the head of this block, and this block does not say where any of them stands. The eleven inherited debts are carried and none is taken, none is repaired and none is rerouted, and three of the five debts above are paid in one cell each with their first editions preserved and two are declined with the reason published. The seventh form was not made and was not guessed at, and the Bare Month was given nothing. And no controller file was touched.**


## DECISIONS OF RECORD, VOLUME 12 — ADDED AND AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 12 OUTLINE PHASE, in its own words

**The Volume 11 close left four decisions open and took four of its own, and the Volume 11 outline phase took seven more, and this block is the seventh set this volume inherited and the seventh it added, and the whole of it is in `outline/volume-12.md`, which is the plan and not this block. THIS BLOCK IS THE SUMMARY AND THE SUMMARY IS NOT THE PLAN, and a writer of Volume 12 reads `outline/volume-12.md` and not this file, and this file is here so that a reader of this document in a year can see what was decided, when, and what it cost.**

**AND THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK IS NOT, repeated from the close record and from the two blocks above it, because a decision block is a page a writer reads and this file is the second such page: the eleven held things a writer must not be handed — the name of the volume before the last and the words the man of about thirty-four says in Chapter 0428; that volume's one rule, the panel of day 438; the answer spoken in Chapter 0357; the refusal spoken in Chapter 0393; the day-154 sentence; the count of chapters carrying the day-154 beat, which the Volume 09 plan withdrew; the words the man of about twenty-seven speaks in Chapter 0486; and the three things belonging to Volume 11 itself, which are the words of its one rule, the name it gave itself and the word it turned on. None of them is printed here and none of them is paraphrased here and no sentence here certifies that any of them is in no file, because the measurement of the hold is at `state/volume-11-close.md` §7.11, with the file paths, the counts, the length of each span and the form each span was taken in, and no span, and two of its rows stand there as disagreements rather than resolved and are published here by reference only. The hold is on each phase's own files and not on where a string stands: a plan is not a page, a permanent witness section is not a page, and a batch record is not a page either. AND THIS PHASE DID NOT RE-RUN THAT MEASUREMENT, BECAUSE RE-RUNNING IT REQUIRES PRINTING THE SPANS AND THIS BLOCK MAY NOT PRINT ONE, AND A PHASE THAT COULD NOT MEASURE THE HOLD AND SAID SO IS BETTER THAN A PHASE THAT MEASURED IT AND LEAKED IT.**

**DECISION ONE, INHERITED AND CARRIED — THE VOICE, CONFIRMED FOR THE ELEVENTH TIME, and it now carries eight instrument rules and not three, and the eighth was added by the phase behind this one and is the one that governs every figure in the plan this block summarises.** Fifty-day volumes, one chapter to one day, about a page, a yard and a wage in salt. Nobody gets stronger. The antagonist is a method and not a form. The only instrument this book has that a return cannot be filled in from is a blank. No narrator frame, and the narrator may not say *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch* or *the reader*. The house figures a Volume 12 batch measures against are published in the plan and not reprinted, **and the mean is a consequence and not a plan and no volume may read its own mean as a quality figure and no review may certify or fail this volume on one. THE NEW EIGHTH RULE IS THIS: A FIGURE THAT REPRODUCES ON ONE OF ITS PARTS AND NOT ON THE OTHERS IS THE SAME KIND OF FIGURE AS ONE THAT REPRODUCED ON NONE, and every row this phase re-derived says what reproduced and what did not, and five of them reproduced and four did not and all nine are published.**

**What it costs, plainly. The series keeps its voice and gets a plan whose own measurements are auditable against the pages that produced them, and it pays for it with four published disagreements between this phase's figures and a close record's on the same corpus, which a reader may now check in about ten minutes and which a plan that published only what agreed with its own inheritance would not have let them check at all.**

**DECISION TWO, TAKEN BY THIS PHASE — THE LINE, audited on five hundred and forty-nine chapter files before it was adopted, with the instrument and the file count published, and the count is 549 and not 499 and it was re-derived in this run and not inherited.**

**A figure of this city went out of this city on a form with one place under a heading and no place under the heading for the person who counted it, and it is right, and nobody four hundred miles off can be shown who made it. A sheet comes up the road with a heading and one place under the heading, and this time the one place is already filled, with a figure of this city that this city did not make. And a room over a market is going to decide what a figure of this city has to carry with it to be believed, and the answer is going to be a line of words, and a line of words under a number is not a description of the number.**

**AND THE HALF THE LINE IS STANDING ON, SAID OUT LOUD, BEFORE A CHAPTER EXISTS, and it is said again here for the fifth volume running and it is the sentence the plan says: THE LINE IS STANDING ON THE THINGS AND NOT ON THE WORDS.** Every load-bearing noun of it is in the high column of this manuscript's own vocabulary, and a line built on nouns in the high column is a line about the oldest thing in this book, and this plan is not embarrassed about that and does not dress it up as novelty. The word column is one word wide, it is at zero on all five hundred and forty-nine chapter files on both case readings and in the singular and in the plural, it is ordinary, it is in the local register, **AND IT IS GOING TO BE SPENT ON A PAGE IN A MOUTH IN A ROOM IN DAYLIGHT WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, AND THIS BLOCK SAYS SO BEFORE A CHAPTER EXISTS INSTEAD OF LETTING A CLOSE FIND IT AFTERWARDS, WHICH IS WHAT THE VOLUME BEFORE THE ONE BEFORE THIS COST.**

**AND THE FOUR THINGS THE MANUSCRIPT HAS NEVER HAD ON A PAGE, named so that a writer can write one and a reviewer can fail it: a figure of this place made in an office nine hundred miles off, in a form that came DOWN the road, into a room where every figure of this place anybody can show is one this city made itself; a line of words written under a figure, on a surface anybody can walk past, saying what the figure is a figure of; a room in which about nine people mean four different things by one ordinary word and no two of them are wrong and the word goes on a page anyway; and a tally-board lying face down in a wharf yard with a figure of this city on the side the sun does not get to, turned face up, and about four people a day walking past a number that has been unreadable for the whole of this flood.**

**What it costs, plainly.
